         ==================================================================
             JDA SOFTWARE SERVICES INC. - MERCHANDISE MANAGEMENT SYSTEM
                                O V E R V I E W
         ==================================================================
                           Operates on: IBM AS/400
                  Languages written in: RPG400 & CL
         The JDA Merchandise Management System was developed to assist in prov-
         iding managers of medium sized retail operations, particularly those
         serving the hard lines market, with up to date and accurate information
         presented in a manner that directly addresses the needs of the
         contemporary merchant, and at the same time provide proper operational
         information to serve the accounting and control aspects of the
         business.
         Modules:
              Financial:
                          Accounts Receivable - Commercial Accounts
                                                Special Orders
                                                Co-op Advertising
                          Accounts Payable    - Cash Requirements
                                                Vendor Payment Management
                                                Vendor History
                                                Invoice Tracking & Control
                          General Ledger      - Budgeting
                                                Retail Stock Ledger
                                                Report Writer
                          Sales Audit         - Cash Control
                                                Inventory Interface
                                                Financial Interface
                                 MEMBER: DOCOVER                 PAGE:   2
              Store Operations:
                          Item Management     - Price Management
                                              - Price Lookup
                                              - Item Maintenance (Store Level)
                          Point of Sale       - Cash Register Interface
                                              - Transaction Logging/Posting
                                              - Sales Audit Interface
                          Store Inventory     - Transfers In/Out
                                              - Receipts, RTV's
                                              - Sales Interface
                                              - Count System
                                              - Physical Counts
              Distribution:
                          Warehouse Operation - Receiving
                                              - Putaway
                                              - Shipping
                                              - RTV's
                                              - Transfers
                                              - Locator
                                              - Picking
                          Store Level         - Receiving
                                              - Shipping
                                              - RTV's
                                              - Transfers
                                              - Locator
                                              - Picking
                                 MEMBER: DOCOVER                 PAGE:   3
              Merchandising:
                          Purchase Orders     - Preprints (Store Level)
                                              - Central P.O.
                                              - PO Management Reporting
                                              - Auto Replinishment
                          Price Change        - Permanent Price Change
                                              - Ad Planning & Tracking
                                              - Markdown Reporting
                          Sales Analysis      - Sales Inquiry-Sales Performance
                                                             -Sales/Customer
                                                             -Sales/Payroll Hour
                                                             -Cust's/Payroll Hr.
                                                             -Customers
                                                             -Gross Margin
                                                             -Accumulative Sales
                                               -SKU Level Historical Inquiry
                                               -Sales Reporting
                          Inventory Control    -Transaction Processing
                                               -Stock Status
                                               -Item Velocity
                                               -Inventory Movement
                                               -Period End Reporting
                          Hard Lines Specific  -Special Orders
                                               -Commodity Products
                                               -Rack Jobbers
              MIS:

                                 MEMBER: DOCMENU                 PAGE:   2
         =======================================================================
                       J D A   S O F T W A R E   S E R V I C E S
                                MENU DRIVER OVERVIEW
         =======================================================================
                              Purpose of Menu Driver:
                              =======================
         -Force Standardization of Menu Presentation.
         -Easy entry,maintenance, and display of HELP Text.
         -Simplify Security through sign-on and menu-level password.
         -Log running of sensitive programs.
         -Allow menus to be changed without affecting current users.
         -Vastly improve the productivity of programmers over traditional
          methods of building menus.
         -Simplify programmer productivity aids.
                                 MEMBER: DOCMENU                 PAGE:   3
         =======================================================================
                                     Features
                                     ========
         -Menus are file driven by a set of programs.
         -Menu files are easily changed by programmers.
         -Menus can be changed even while users are using them.
         -Help text can be attached to each menu item.
         -Help text is file driven, not programmed.
         -Help text is easily created and changed.
         -Help text is easily displayed by users.
         -Menus can be printed on paper, with or without printing the programs
          that each menu runs, and its parameters, and with or without printing
          the associated help text for each menu item.
         -Menu printing can print all menus, or printing can start at a
          particular menu, and the program will print all menus that are
          called from or below that menu.
         -The order of printing of menus can be established by the user.
         -Menu Driver system uses a particular Job Description, which can
          be easily established by a programmer.
         -The sign-on program for each user profile is contained in a file.
         -The default outq and jobq is contained in a file and varies by
          workstation i.d. This gives the advantage of all print-outs, whether
          done directly from the workstation, or submitted (using the JDA
          submit utilities), going to the printer defined for that workstation.
         -Any submitted jobs (using the JDA submit utilties) will use the
          user-profile default job description.
         -Submitted jobs can be restricted to a particular JOBQ by changing
          the default job description.
         -Message switching is simplified by using the HELP key off of any
          menu. Messages can be sent easily to any workstation described to
          the menu driver system.
                                 MEMBER: DOCMENU                 PAGE:   4
         =======================================================================
                                     Commands
                                     ========
            MENOPR             Displays the JDA System Operator Menu on either
                               the console or any workstation. This program is
                               usually custom-written for each installation.
            MENU    Menuname   Displays the menu name passed as paramater.
                               If no paramater is used, 'MASTER' is the
                               default.
                               If menu name does not exist, program ends.
            MENUKEEP           Creates Library 'JDAMENU' with copies of menu
                               files. ** WARNING ** Deletes any existing
                               library called 'JDAMENU'
            MENUCOPY           Allows menus to be copied from 'JDAMENU'
                               into current menu files. Allows complete
                               menus or individual items from menus.
                                 MEMBER: DOCMENU                 PAGE:   5
         =======================================================================
                           M E N U   M A N A G E M E N T
                           =============================
         The System Control File: One file exists, with one record in it
                                  which contains the name that will appear
                                  on the top of every menu, and on most
                                  screen inquiries and reports produced by
                                  JDA programmers. To change this file,
                                  display the control menu (MENU MENCTL)
                                  Run option number 1.
                                  Change the data on the screen.
         Creating or changing a menu:
         The first step in creating or changing a menu is to access the
         maintenance program. This can be accomplished by displaying the
         System Control Menu (MENU MENCTL) ans running option 2.
         OR - Display any menu and access the maintenance program from the Help
              screen, and follow the following instructions.
         To access one of the menus enter the following from a command screen.
                       MENU menuname
         Press 'HELP' which will display the help functions. Familiarize
         yourself with the HELP options.
         To change or create a menu, from the 'HELP' menu press CMD 24.
         This will display the menu you are currently at. If you are changing
         this menu, press enter. Otherwise, enter in the name of a new menu
         or the one you want to change.
         Enter the Menu heading information:
         Enter the Menu Title the password (Note: the user profiles QPGMR
         and QSECOFR will not ask for passwords) if required, the hierarchy
         (devise your own hierarchy system, the menu print programs sort
         menus by this hierarchy), and the maximum number of entries (12 or
         less entries will center on the screen. More than 12 will use the
         1st 15 on the left, and the next 15 on the right.)
                                 MEMBER: DOCMENU                 PAGE:   6
         =======================================================================
         Enter the item number you want to add or change:
         Enter the Menu Text, program to call, and paramaters one and two,
         if necessary. Indicate with a 'Y' or 'N' whether or not you wish
         to have the menu item logged. If an item is to be logged, the
         Menu Driver program (MEMDRV) will write a record to this file every
         time the program is run. The log file can be printed by selecting
         the correct option of of the Control Menu (MENU MENCTL).
         Also enter any warning message numbers that will be displayed
         (maximum of two) whenever the program is run. These warning messages
         can be entered or changed by using program MSGMNT, which is number
         16 on the programmer's menu (MENU MENPGM).
         The next screen will prompt for Help Text for that menu item. Please
         fill in the Help Text now, because experience shows that it won't
         get done otherwise.
                                 MEMBER: DOCMENU                 PAGE:   7
         =======================================================================
                       ADDING USERS AND MANAGING DEFAULTS
                       ==================================
         User Profile and Workstation Defaults.
                1. Display the Control Menu (MENU MENCTL).
                2. Run option number 3 to add to the user profile
                   file. For each user profile to use the system,
                   enter the user profile name, press enter, and
                   enter the default (sign on) menu name, and the
                   default job description. A menu name of *NONE
                   will give the command screen when the SIGNON
                   program is run.
                   The default job description will be used in all
                   submitted JDA jobs. Standard is 'JDAJOBD'.
                3. Run option number 4 to add to the workstation file.
                   Enter the workstation I.D., press enter, and enter
                   the default menu name, the default print queue,
                   the store number (if using JDA Merchandise Package)
                   and the description of the workstation. The
                   description is used in the message switching
                   programs (used by pressing HELP off any menu)
                   The workstation address can also be entered here
                   but is used for documentation purposes only.
         Changing the User Profile:
         Change the user profile to call the initial program
                          'SIGNON.PGMLIB'
         Changing the Library List and JDAJOBD (Job Description)
         Change the program 'JDALIBL' residing in CTLSRC.PGMSRC,
         changing the RPLLIBL statement, the CHGJOBD statement
         for both JDAJOBD and JDASDAD.
         Job description JDASDAD is used only in command SD.
                                 MEMBER: DOCMENU                 PAGE:   8
         =======================================================================
                           THE OPTIONS FILE
                           ================
         Throughout the Application Documentation for any JDA Application
         Packages, reference will be made to the 'OPTIONS' file.
         This file is a file, keyed by 'OPTION NUMBER' that contains
         various options and defaults used by the application programs.
         An example of an option is 'Account Number to Credit in A/P
         Check Run.' The purpose is to allow the programmer to customize
         applications, but allow easy changing of the options when the
         software is installed in another account, and to allow the user
         to have some flexibility in managing options.
         There is an option on the System Control Menu (MENU MENCTL) that
         will allow the maintanence of these options. The program will also
         allow the printing of the entire option file by pressin the
         print key from the first screen. For more examples of options
         and their uses, print the file.
                                   *** END OF REPORT ***
                                 MEMBER: DOCSYS                  PAGE:   1
                                 MEMBER: DOCSYS                  PAGE:   2
         =======================================================================
                       J D A   S O F T W A R E   S E R V I C E S
                                MENU DRIVER OVERVIEW
         =======================================================================
         *** WARNING ***
         This document describes the Menu Driver, including instructions on
         how to display and change menu-level passwords. Do not leave this
         document in an un-secure place. If your security requirements require,
         remove this member from the system.
         The member is:     Library:  CTLSRC
                        Source File:  HELPSRC
                             Member:  DOCSYS
         Purpose of Menu Driver:
         =======================
         Force Standardization of Menu Presentation.
         Easy entry,maintenance, and display of HELP Text.
         Simplify Security through sign-on and menu-level password.
         Log running of sensitive programs.
         Allow menus to be changed without affecting current users.
         Vastly improve the productivity of programmers over traditional
         methods of building menus.
         Simplify programmer productivity aids.
                                 MEMBER: DOCSYS                  PAGE:   3
         Features (Menu Driver):
         =======================
         Menus are file driven by a set of programs.
         Menu files are easily changed by programmers.
         Menus can be changed even while users are using them.
         Help text can be attached to each menu item.
         Help text is file driven, not programmed.
         Help text is easily created and changed.
         Help text is easily displayed by users.
         Menus can be printed on paper, with or without printing the programs
         that each menu runs, and its parameters, and with or without printing
         the associated help text for each menu item.
         Menu printing can print all menus, or printing can start at a
         particular menu, and the program will print all menus that are
         called from or below that menu.
         The order of printing of menus can be established by the user.
         Menu Driver system uses a particular Job Description, which can
         be easily established by a programmer.
         The sign-on program for each user profile is contained in a file.
         The default outq and jobq is contained in a file and varies by
         workstation i.d. This gives the advantage of all print-outs, whether
         done directly from the workstation, or submitted (using the JDA
         submit utilities), going to the printer defined for that workstation.
         Any submitted jobs (using the JDA submit utilties) will use the
         user-profile default job description.
         Submitted jobs can be restricted to a particular JOBQ by changing
         the default job description.
         Message switching is simplified by using the HELP key off of any
         menu. Messages can be sent easily to any workstation described to
         the menu driver system.
                                 MEMBER: DOCSYS                  PAGE:   4
         Features (Productivity aids):
         =============================
         A default source file, program-object library, physical file library,
         and logical file library can be established for each workstation.
         A number of commands exist to simplify programming:
         Some of the programs can be run from the programmers menu (MENU MENPGM)
         but most programmers operate from a command screen.
            Command Paramater  Function
            ======= =========  ===========================================
            CALC               Calls the JDA Calculator. (Try it)
            CAT     Filename   DSPFD filename *MBRLIST
            CATALOG Library    Lists all files and their members from a
                               library. The library name will be prompted
                               for if blank
            CF      ---        Simplifies copying of one file to another.
            CLEANUP ---        Runs the following commands: Clroutq qprint2
                                                            Clroutq qpjoblog
                                                            Clroutq qpsrvdmp
            DLTRRN  Cmd 4      Deletes a range of relative record numbers from
                               any file
            DQ                 Display an outq
                               Parms:   ME        Workstation default outq
                                        *         Qprint
                                        J         Qpjoblog
                                        2         Qprint2
                                        S         Qprints
                                       ' '        All outq's in library list
                                 MEMBER: DOCSYS                  PAGE:   5
            LF      filename   Runs SEU then CRTLF
            LIST80             Prompts for name of, and lists 1st 80 bytes
                               of 1st 500 records of any physical file.
            LS                 Prompts for name of, and lists any source file
                               member.
            CS      Member     Prompts for name of, and copies any source
                               member from one source file to another.
            MENOPR             Displays the JDA System Operator Menu on either
                               the console or any workstation. This program is
                               usually custom-written for each installation.
            MENU    Menuname   Displays the menu name passed as paramater.
                               If no paramater is used, 'MASTER' is the
                               default.
                               If menu name does not exist, program ends.
            LIBRARY ---        Allows a library to be saved or restored from
                               or to diskette or tape.
            MENUKEEP           Creates Library 'JDAMENU' with copies of menu
                               files. ** WARNING ** Deletes any existing
                               library called 'JDAMENU'
            MENUCOPY           Allows menus to be copied from 'JDAMENU'
                               into current menu files. Allows complete
                               menus or individual items from menus.
            PASSTHRU           Calls the passthru command to allow signon to
                               the System/36 (JDA Office only).
            OFF                Signoff
                                 MEMBER: DOCSYS                  PAGE:   6
            Command Paramater  Function
            ======= =========  ===========================================
            ORGANIZE Libname   Pass the name of any library name to this
                               command, and the program will determine the
                               total number of deleted records in each
                               member within each file, and if the total
                               deleted records exceeds 3% of the total
                               records, the file will be reorganized,
                               using the RGZPFM command.
            PF      Filename   Runs SEU then CRTPF
            PRINTER Device     Starts a Printer and assigns an Outq to it.
            SC      Membername Runs SEU then submits a program which will
                               delete the program, if it exists, and
                               do a CRTCLPGM.
            SD      Membername Runs SEU then submits a program which will
                               delete the file, if it exists, and
                               do a CRTDSPF.
            SR      Membername Runs SEU then submits a program which will
                               delete the program, if it exists, and
                               do a CRTRPGPGM.
            SDA     Membername Runs DSNFMT
            SEU     Membername Runs Edtsrc. Option exists to change the
                               workstation defaults which are:
                               Default Source File
                               Default Program Library (for CL,RPG,DSPF objects)
                               Default library for logical files.
                               Default library for physical files.
            SUBMIT  Pgmname    Submits the first paramater, using the
                               workstation default job description.
            XREF               Allows the display of the program/file cross
                               reference file, as of the last time the
                               program/file cross reference listing was done.
         OTHER PROGRAMS:
         See the programmer's menu (MENU MENPGM) and the Control Menu
         (MENU MENCTL) for other productivity aids.
                                 MEMBER: DOCSYS                  PAGE:   7

                      INSTALLING THE JDA MENU DRIVER
                      ==============================
              1. Load PGMLIB.
              2. Load PGMSRC if source is to be given.
              3. Determine the Library list to be used.
              4. Create the JDA job description (CRTJDAJOBD pgm)
              5. Change JDALIBL.
              6. Add jobq JDAJOBQ (Maxact 1).
              7. Create, or add user profiles as described previously.
              8. Delete all menus except MENPGM,MENCTL,MASTER,
                 and those used in purchased applications.
              9. Change MENU MASTER.
             10. Peruse all menus for custom entries from last install.

                                 MEMBER: DOCAP2                  PAGE:   1
         ==================================================================
               A C C O U N T S   P A Y A B L E   A P P L I C A T I O N
                                O V E R V I E W
         ==================================================================
                           Operates on: IBM as/400
                  Languages written in: RPG400 & CL
                       First Installed: 1981.
         ==================================================================
                                      FEATURES
         ==================================================================
         *  Features an open item system designed for a multi-location
            retail operation.
         *  Provides support for multiple companies.
         *  Provides an automatic interface with the JDA General Ledger
            Package, which supports the Retail Method of Accounting.
         *  Provides automatic posting to the Accounts Payable Control
            Account, Bank Account and Cash Discount Account.
         *  Provides for an extensive Vendor File with a six digit
            Vendor Number.
         *  Provides a vendor file alpha search wherever the vendor number
            is required.
         *  Supports miscellaneous vendors.
         *  Provides a comprehensive Terms Code management system.
         *  Invoice input is batch total controlled and can be done from
            any number of workstations simultaneously.
         *  Provides different entry modules for Trade, Freight and
            Expense Payables.
         *  Supports Unmatched File reporting for Trade Payables.
         *  Provides a Freight Invoice reporting sub-system.
         *  Allows inquiry on Unmatched, Open and Paid Invoices.
         *  Provides extensive check selection criteria.
                                 MEMBER: DOCAP2                  PAGE:   2
         ==================================================================
                                  FEATURES - cont'd
         ==================================================================
         *  Allows separate check runs for Freight, Expense and Trade payables
            or any combination in one check run.
         *  Pre-paid invoices can be processed and will be included on the next
            check register without producing a check.
         *  Provides holdback management on individual invoices.
         *  Keeps a check reconciliation file.
         *  Allows open invoices to be cancelled, and reverses the original
            accounting.
         *  Allows all invoices on a check to be purged from the History File.
         *  Allows individual invoices to be purged from the History File.
                                 MEMBER: DOCAP2                  PAGE:   3
         ==================================================================
                          STANDARD REPORTS & INQUIRIES
         ==================================================================
           Screen Inquiries:
           -----------------
           AP0002     Vendor Alpha Search
           AP0003     Vendor Status/Performance
           AP0009     General Invoice Inquiry - Open
                                              - Unmatched
                                              - History (Paid)
           AP0019     Display Terms Codes
           AP0046     Paid invoice inquiry by check number
           AP0055     Paid invoice inquiry by P.O./Voucher number
           AP0077     Inquiry of "HELD" Vendors
           AP0095     Vendor Factor alpha search
           Standard Reports:
           -----------------
           AP0020     Vendor Listing by Clerk Number
           AP0044     Vendor Listing by Department Number
           AP0017     Extensive Open Payables Reporting
                       - Cash Requirements by discount/pay date
                       - Aged invoices by invoice date
                       - Any logical combination of Expense,
                         Freight and Trade (Open and Unmatched)
                       - All or selected vendor(s)
                       - All vendors for a selected clerk
           AP0047     A/P History for selected vendor, within
                         user supplied date parameters
           AP0043     A/P Summary listing, totalling all vendor
                         invoices in History file
           AP0064     Unmatched Listing by Department used for
                         period end accrual.
           In addition, all entry is proof listed and the check run
           provides the user with an extensive Check Register, detailed
           and summary.
         ==================================================================
                             SUBPROGRAMS USED
         ==================================================================
           GL0026     Select a company.
           GL0502     Prompt for memo posting period.

                   ¦                     ¦                     ¦
             .............         .............         .............
              .           .         .           .         .           .
               . APUMCH    .         . APOPEN    .         . APHIST    .
              .           .         .           .         .           .

             .............                    .............
              .           .                    .           .
               . APATRD    .                    . APADST    .  (Trade Only)
              .           .                    .           .
             .............                    .............
                                 MEMBER: DOCAP2                  PAGE:   5
         ==================================================================
                           DATA BASE OVERVIEW  - cont'd
         ==================================================================
         Table Files:
         ------------
          APTERM      Terms Code Table
                      Contains valid terms codes, description of the terms
                      code and information necessary to calculate the
                      discount amount and the discount date.
          APCRCD      Credit Codes Table
                      Contains credit codes with their descriptions, account
                      number, whether it is a debit or credit, whether it is
                      subject to a discount and default department.
          OPTIONS     Special Processing Option File
          TBLSTR      Table of Valid Stores
          INVDPT      Table of valid departments, sub-departments
         Master Files:
         -------------
          APSUPP      Vendor File
         Transaction Files:
         ------------------
          APOPEN      Open (Unpaid) Invoice File
                      Contains all open invoices and relevant
                      information entered into the system.
                      Purged when invoice paid in the check run.
          APADST      Invoice Distribution File
                      Contains invoice and account distribution data
                      for trade payables (used in the Purchase Journal).
          APUMCH      Unmatched Invoice File
                      Holding file of trade invoices that have not
                      yet been matched.  Purged when invoices moved
                      to APOPEN as part of the matching process.
          APMISC      Miscellaneous Vendor File
                      Contains miscellaneous vendor name and
                      address.  Purged when invoice is paid.
          APCREC      Check Reconciliation File
                      Contains one record for each uncleared check.
                                 MEMBER: DOCAP2                  PAGE:   6
         ==================================================================
                           DATA BASE OVERVIEW  - cont'd
         ==================================================================
         History Files:
         --------------
          APHIST    (APOPEN)  A/P History File
                              File of paid invoices. Purged on demand
                              by invoice date.
          APFHST    (APOPEN)  A/P Freight History File
                              File of paid freight invoices - used in the
                              freight reporting sub-system.  Purged on
                              demand by invoice date.
         Workstation Files:
         ------------------
          APITRD      Temporary Trade Invoice File
                      Contains invoice information for trade invoices
          APATRD      Temporary Trade Invoice Distribution File
                      Contains account distribution information for
                      trade payables invoices.
          APIEXP      Temporary Expense Invoice File
                      Contains invoice information for expense invoices
          APAEXP      Temporary Expense Trade Invoice Distribution File
                      Contains account distribution information for
                      expense payables invoices.
          APIFRT      Temporary Freight Invoice File
                      Contains invoice information for freight invoices
          APAFRT      Temporary Freight Invoice Distribution File
                      Contains account distribution information for
                      freight payables invoices.
          Work Files:
          -----------
          APBTCH      Workstation/Application Batch Control File.
                      Contains the batch control totals for a specific
                      workstation/application and whether or not there
                      are errors present.
          APCHEK      Check Run Detail File.
                      Contains the invoice information for the check run.
          APCHK#      A/P Check Number File
                      Contains the check numbers, corresponding serial
                      number and if the check should be voided for every
                      check in the check run.
                                 MEMBER: DOCAP2                  PAGE:   7
         ==================================================================
                           DATA BASE OVERVIEW  - cont'd
         ==================================================================
          Work Files: - cont'd
          -----------
          APCHKW      A/P Check Run Workfile
                      Contains vendor selection criteria for the check run.
          APCRED      A/P Credit File
                      Contains the invoices totalling a credit in the
                      check run.
          WORKFILE    Workfile
                                 MEMBER: DOCAP2                  PAGE:   8

                                 MEMBER: DOCAP2                  PAGE:  44
         =======================================================================
                             MAJOR PROGRAM DESCRIPTIONS
         =======================================================================
         APT001     Enter/Update Trade Payables
                    This program allows you to enter/update Trade Payables
                    Invoices.  This is a batch total controlled process.
                    If the vendor entered has a default terms code set up,
                    the program will calculate the discount date, discount
                    amount and discount percentage for the user. An invoice
                    can be for one store or can be distributed among many
                    stores.  There is also the capability to span multiple
                    companies.
         APF001/                 Freight/
         APX001     Enter/Update Expense Payables
                                                             Freight/
                    This program allows you to enter/update  Expense  Payables
                    Invoices.  This is a batch total controlled process.  An
                    invoice can be for one store or can be distributed among
                    many stores.  There is also the capability to span multiple
                    companies.
         APF003/         Freight/
         APX003     Post Expense Payables to Open
                    Before this step can be processed, the user must produce
                    an error free listing of their batch.  Once an error free
                    listing has been obtained, this step will write the APOPEN
                    records from the workstation files.  This step also writes
                    the journal entry by creating a GLJENT record.
         APT003     Post Trade Payables to the Unmatched File
                    Before this step can be processed, the user must produce
                    an error free listing of their batch.  Once an error free
                    listing has been obtained, this step will write records
                    from the Entry Workstation File to the Unmatched File.
                    The workstation file is then cleared, the batch total
                    file is cleared, and entry of the next batch can begin.
         APT004     Matching of A/P Trade Unmatched Payables
                    This process will allow users to update records in the
                    Unmatched Invoice File (APUMCH) for the purpose of matching
                    and posting to the Open Payables file so that they may be
                    paid.
                                 MEMBER: DOCAP2                  PAGE:  45
         =======================================================================
                           MAJOR PROGRAM DESCRIPTIONS - cont'd
         =======================================================================
         APT006     Post the Matched A/P Trade from the Unmatched File to Open
                    Before this step can be processed, the user must produce
                    an error free listing of their batch.  Once an error free
                    listing has been obtained, this step will write the APOPEN
                    records from the Unmatched (APUMCH ) File.  This step will
                    also write the journal entry by creating a GLJENT record.
         APCHEK01   Check Run Selection Criteria
                    1. Select Company to be paid.
                    2. Enter Bank Account to Credit.
                    3. Select any combination of: a) Trade Vendors
                                                  b) Expense Vendors
                                                  c) Freight Vendors
                    4. Enter Argument Date & Discount Date.
                        Any invoices with a discount date between the argument
                        date and the discount date will be selected for payment
                        and any available discount taken.
                        Any invoice with a pay date equal to or less than the
                        argument date will be selected for payment.
                        * Note: Each vendor can have the following codes in
                                their Vendor Master Record:
                                Push Discount Days: There is an overall default
                                                    in the 'OPTIONS' file, but
                                                    this field can be varied by
                                                    vendor. The selection
                                                    program will add this many
                                                    days to the discount date to
                                                    'PUSH' the vendor to his
                                                    ultimate tolerance for
                                                    taking discounts.
                                Take Discount Y/N : Irregardless of whether the
                                                    discount date is past, take
                                                    the discount anyways.
                    5. Maintain the 'HOLD' status of vendors - put vendors
                        on or off 'HOLD'. If they are on hold, they will not be
                        selected for payment until they are taken off hold.
                    6. Select 'ALL' vendors, based on vendor types previously
                        selected (Freight, Expense, Trade).
                    7. Select individual vendors, or if 'ALL' has been selected,
                        remove certain vendors from the selection file.
                    8. Modify the argument date, discount date, and Take
                        Discount Y/N codes for selected vendors, which apply for
                        this check run only.
                    9. Enter the check date (can be any date) and the starting
                        check number. The 'OPTIONS' file determines whether the
                        the program is assigning the check numbers, or the
                        starting check number is to be input.
                                 MEMBER: DOCAP2                  PAGE:  46
         =======================================================================
                           MAJOR PROGRAM DESCRIPTIONS - cont'd
         =======================================================================
         APCHEK02   Produce a Check Register
                    A summary check register, showing one line per check, and
                    a detailed check register are produced.  The selection
                    programs and check registers can be run any number of times
                    until the desired result is produced.
         APCHEK03   Print the Checks
                    The checks are printed on-line and the user is prompted
                    to either update the files ("CONTINUE") or cancel the
                    update ("CANCEL").
         APCHEK04   Update the Files for the Check Run
                    First, every record that was processed in the check run
                    is written from Open (APOPEN) into History (APHIST). Then
                    the check reconciliation file is updated and the check
                    run journal entry is written.  Finally, the A/P balances
                    in APOPEN are re-calculated.
         AP0030     Select Invoices for Payment
                    Using the vendors selected (APCHKW) and the Open Payables
                    File (APOPEN), this program writes invoices selected to
                    the APCHEK file.  The selection criteria is complex, based
                    on whether the invoice or vendor is on hold, whether push
                    dates are involved, etc.  Basically, if there is a discount
                    available, and the discount date is within the range of
                    argument date to discount date, select for payment and take
                    discount.  Otherwise, set discount amounts to zero, and if
                    the payment date is less that the argument date, select for
                    payment.
         AP0037     Remove the Paid Invoices from APOPEN and write to APHIST
                    Writes all paid invoices from APOPEN to APHIST and deletes
                    the APOPEN record.
         AP0038     Writes Check Reconciliation File and Interfaces to G/L
                    Writes one record to the check reconciliation file (APCREC)
                    for each check and creates a GLJENT record for the Check
                    Run.
                                 MEMBER: DOCAP2                  PAGE:  47
         =======================================================================
                           MAJOR PROGRAM DESCRIPTIONS - cont'd
         =======================================================================
         AP0016     Prompt for A/P Open Reporting
                    Prompts for report type (Cash Requirements or Aged A/P),
                    dates to age by (up to 6), Vendor types to include and
                    one vendor or *ALL.
         AP0018     Decides on Selection Criteria for Open Payables Reporting
                    Writes a Limits Record over APOPUML1 for Report Program.
         AP0017     Open Payables Report Program
                    Using APOPUML1, Writes either an Aged A/P Report, Cash
                    Requirements Report or Vendor Debit Balances Report
                    in Detail (One line per Invoice) or Summary (One line
                    per Vendor) form.
         AP0071     Select Records to be Included in the Purchase Journal
                    Select those records from APHIST with a specified
                    department (from OPTIONS) and writes them to a workfile
                    called WRKPJ in either DEPT/STORE order or STORE/DEPT order.
         AP0072
         AP0073     Purchase Journal Report
                    Takes the records from WRKPJ and calculates the Retail
                    amount by refering to the Vendor/Department Factor File
                    (APDEW). The Gross Margin Percentage is calculated and
                    percentages over a maximum or under a minimum (as stated
                    in the program) or marked on the report with an "*".
                    AP0072 prints the report in Store/Dept order, AP0073 prints
                    the report in Dept/Store order.  Detail and Summary reports
                    are available.
                                   *** END OF REPORT ***
                                 MEMBER: DOCGL1                  PAGE:   1
         ==================================================================
                G E N E R A L   L E D G E R   A P P L I C A T I O N
                                O V E R V I E W
         ==================================================================
                           Operates on: IBM AS/400
                  Languages written in: RPG400 & CL
                       First Installed: 1981.
         ==================================================================
                                FEATURES
         ==================================================================
         *  Balance forward system, with monthly close outs.
         *  Supports 12 or 13 periods per year.
         *  Supports the Retail Method of Accounting.
         *  Provides support for multiple companies with separate close outs
            for each company.
         *  Provides easy interface from other systems with data being checked
            for program-detectable errors.
         *  Provides for entry of manual journal entries.
         *  Provides for user defined periodic journal entries to be generated
            on a straight-line or declining balance basis through the Automatic
            Journal Entries System.
         *  Provides for recurring journal entries to be saved so that only the
            dollar amounts need to be maintained every period through the
            Recurring Journal Entries System.
         *  Posting allowed to any period in the current year.
         *  Keeps transaction detail for current period, current year, and
            last year.
         *  Provides easy maintenance of the chart of accounts.
              Account Number Structure - XXX Company
                                         XXX Major Account Number (Department)
                                         XXX Minor Account Number (Sub-Department)
                                         XXX Store Number
         *  Provides a flexible G/L report writer.
                                 MEMBER: DOCGL1                  PAGE:   2
         ==================================================================

         ==================================================================
           Screen Inquiries:
           -----------------

           GL0018     Detail of account - current period (allows print)
           GL0028                         current year   (allows print)
           GLLY28                         last year      (allows print)
           GL0032     Sum of activity - total per period/account
           GL0034     Detail by Batch - current period (allows print)
           GLY034                       current year   (allows print)

                                 MEMBER: DOCGL1                  PAGE:   3
         ==================================================================
                                   REPORT WRITER
         ==================================================================
           Due to the requirement that the user be allowed to design reports
           without the aid of a programmer, and because of the unpredictable
           and varying request for different formats of financial statements,
           the report writer programs were designed to allow the user to
           create custom reports.
           Features of the Report Writer:
           ------------------------------
           *  The user has the ability to create any number of reports.
           *  The user indicates which account balances are to be put into each
              line of the report.
           *  The user defines the column headings and has the ability to
              control the detail line descriptions.
           *  Up to 26 different report formats, each of which can have up to
              eight combinations of the following columns:
                Available Columns:
                    Current                      Year-to-Date
                    -------                      ------------
                    Actual                       Actual
                    Budget                       Budget
                    Last Year                    Last Year
                    Difference of Actual         Difference of Actual
                      to Budget                    to Budget
                    Difference of Actual         Difference of Actual
                      to Last Year                 to Last Year
                    Actual as % of Total         Actual as % of Total
                    Budget as % of Total         Budget as % of Total
                    Last Year as % of Total      Last Year as % of Total
                    Variance to Budget           Variance to Budget
                    Variance to Last Year        Variance to Last Year
           *  Provides ability for Quarter-to-Date reporting by requesting
              that either the Current or Year-to-Date data be overridden with
              the Quarter-to-Date data at report run time.
           *  Provides ability for multiple company/multiple store reporting.
                                 MEMBER: DOCGL1                  PAGE:   4

         ==================================================================
                           DATA BASE OVERVIEW  - cont'd
         ==================================================================
         Table Files:
         ------------
          GLCNTL      Company Control File
                      Contains one record for each company that
                      is using the General Ledger.  All files and
                      reports are by company number.  This file
                      contains critical information that must be
                      present for the system to operate properly,
                      such as the number of periods per year(12 or 13).
                      For Companies using 12 months per year,it is
                      critical that the 'COMPANY MONTH LIST' be
                      entered. Starting with the first month of the
                      fiscal year, enter each month as below.
                      NOVDECJANFEBMARAPRMAYJUNJULAUGSEP
                      Failure to do this properly may cause
                      unpredictable results.
                      Each record contains current fiscal year and
                      period for that company.
          GLMAJR      Major Account File
                      Contains one record for each major account.
                      This file also indicates the department, if any,
                      associated with the major account and if it
                      belongs in the retail stock ledger.
          TBLSTR      Store's Table
          OPTIONS     Special Processing Option File
          MENUCTL     System Name File
         Master Files:
         -------------
          GLMSTR      Chart of Accounts File
                      Contains one record for each account number
                      that the company is using.
                      This file also contains account balances.
                      Accounts cannot be deleted from this file
                      if they have any balance, or have had any
                      activity in the current year.
          GLAUTO      Automatic Journal Entries File
                      Contains automatic journal entry data for
                      posting to GLJENT.
          GLMNTH      Current Period File
                      Contains all transactions that have posted
                      from the suspended batch file for the current period.
                                 MEMBER: DOCGL1                  PAGE:   6
         ==================================================================
                           DATA BASE OVERVIEW  - cont'd
         ==================================================================
         Master Files:  cont'd
         -------------
          GLYEAR      Current Year File
                      Same as GLMNTH except contains data for
                      closed out periods for the current year
          GLSUMF      Account Summary File
                      Contains the sum of the activity for each
                      account for each period. Used in the
                      Report Writer system.
          GLCURD      General Ledger Current Date File.
                      Contains the current system wide posting date and the
                      company to roll the G/L date with at month-end.
          GLRCHD      Recurring Journal Entries Header File.
                      Header file for Recurring Journal Entries, used to aid in
                      the selection of suspended batches for entry, update,
                      listing, posting, and deleting.
          GLRCJE      Recurring Journal Entries File
                      Contains recurring journal entry data for
                      posting to GLJENT.
          GLBUDG      Current Year Budget File.
                      Current Year Budget File - used in Report Writer.
          GLBUDGNY    Next Years Budget file.
          GLREPHDR    Report Writer Heading File
                      Contains the user-defined headings.
          GLFMTS      Report Writer Formats File.
                      Contains the user defined report formats.
          GLREPS      Report Writer Control File.
                      Contains the user defined report criteria.
          GLCOSP      Cost of Sales Percents File.
                      Cost of sales percents, created by the Accounts Payable
                      outstanding unmatched analysis programs. Used in the
                      calculation of the Retail Method of Accounting Cost of
                      Sales routine.
          GLROLL
                     Contains the last used rolling number used in assigning
                     member names to files (Batch numbers) in both A/P and
                     G/L.
                                 MEMBER: DOCGL1                  PAGE:   7
         ==================================================================
                           DATA BASE OVERVIEW  - cont'd
         ==================================================================
         Transaction Files:
         ------------------
          GLJENT      Suspended Batch File
                      Contains Journal Entries and Batch files
                      that have data that is to be posted to the
                      G/L. This file is used in the interface from
                      other systems to the G/L, and in fact, is the
                      only way to interface.
                      This is a multi-member file, each member being
                      a different journal entry or batch.
          GLHEAD      Journal Entries Header File.
                      Header file for Journal Entries, used to aid in the
                      selection of suspended batches for entry, update, listing,
                      posting, and deleting.
         History Files:
         --------------
          GLMSTRLY  (GLMSTR)  Copy of GLMSTR taken during year end close
                              out, used for reporting last year data.
          GLLAST    (GLYEAR)  Detail transactions for last year, basically
                              a copy of GLYEAR taken during year end close
                              out.
          GLMNTHLY  (GLMNTH)  Used to simplify the programming of programs
                              that use the last files. This file will always
                              be empty.
          GLBUDGLY  (GLBUDG)  Last Years Budget file.
                              Budget data from last year, a copy of GLBUDG.
          Work Files:
          -----------
          GLSTOK     Retail Stock Ledger File.
                     Work file used in the Retail Stock Ledger programs.

         =======================================================================
                             MAJOR PROGRAM DESCRIPTIONS
         =======================================================================
         GLA001     Enter/Update Automatic Journal Entries
                    This program allows you to enter/update the automatic
                    journal entry information.  This information includes
                    the company number, the automatic J.E. rule (name), the
                    from-to accounts to add up or initial amount, the amount
                    used so far, period to start automatic rule at and the
                    number of periods to take it for or the percentage of the
                    balance to take, and the debit and credit accounts to post
                    to.
         GLR001     Enter/Update Recurring Journal Entries
                    This program allows you to enter/update the header and
                    detail information for recurring journal entries.  The
                    header information includes:  company number (may default),
                    recurring J.E. name, reference and description.  Detail
                    information includes the account number, amount, retail
                    amount, description and if the entry should be reversed
                    next month.
         GL0001     Enter/Update Manual Journal Entries
                    This program allows you to enter/update the header and
                    detail information for manual journal entries.  The
                    header information includes:  company number (may default),
                    reference and description.  Detail information includes
                    the account number, amount, retail amount, description
                    and if the entry should be reversed next month.
         GLA002/
         GLR003     Post Automatic/Recurring Journal Entries to Suspended
                    This program takes the automatic/recurring J.E. information
                    and creates a GLJENT record.  It then calls GL0012 which
                    will create a header record for the batch so it can be
                    viewed, maintained and posted like a manual journal entry.
                                 MEMBER: DOCGL1                  PAGE:  15
         =======================================================================
                          MAJOR PROGRAM DESCRIPTIONS - cont'd
         =======================================================================
         GL0014     Posts a Journal Entry to the Current Month File
                    Called from GLPOST.
                    This program reads the GLJENT records for the batch(es)
                    selected for posting.  If somehow the account is invalid,
                    the program uses a default account from the OPTIONS file,
                    otherwise the balances in the GLMSTR file (Chart of Accounts
                    File) are updated and a GLMNTH record is written.  This
                    program also updates the summary file, GLSUMF, and then
                    deletes the GLJENT record.
         GLMEND     Month-End Close Out
                    In this process the GLMNTH records are written to GLYEAR.
                    If the entry in not to be reversed the GLMNTH record is
                    deleted, otherwise, if the entry is to be reversed, the
                    sign is changed on the amount and retail amount fields,
                    the reverse field (GLTREV = "R" or " ") is blanked out
                    and the record remains in GLMNTH.  The summary file, GLSUMF,
                    is rebuilt at this time.  A message is printed that lets
                    you know that the month-end has been completed for
                    Period XX/Year XXXX.  This program also determines if this
                    is the last period of the year and the year-end close should
                    be called.
         GLYEND     Year-End Close Out
                    In this process the last year data gets cleared out from
                    GLLAST and GLMSTRLY.  Then the new GLLAST gets created by
                    rolling GLYEAR into GLLAST and clearing out GLYEAR.
                    GLMSTRLY, a duplicate of GLMSTR is also created.  Then we
                    print a message that the year end close out for Period XX,
                    Year XXXX has been done.  The summary file, GLSUMF is
                    rebuilt again.
                                 MEMBER: DOCGL1                  PAGE:  16
         =======================================================================
                          MAJOR PROGRAM DESCRIPTIONS - cont'd
         =======================================================================
         GL0036     Create Retail Stock Ledger Files
                    This program uses GLMAJR to decide if the department
                    should be included in the retail stock ledger, and if
                    so, uses GLSUMF to calculate the following amounts
                    for a Cost of Goods Sold Figure:
                        Beginning Inventory
                        Add: Purchases, Net of Returns
                             Freight
                             Markups,Net
                        Net Additions
                        Merch. Available for Sale
                        Less: Net Sales
                              Markdowns,Net
                              Shortage
                              Promo Markdown Estimate
                        Total Retail Reductions
                        Ending Inventory
                        Cost of Goods Sold
                        * Gross Margin
                          Inventory Turns
         GL0035     Inquire into the Retail Stock Ledger
                    Lets you inquire into the retail stock ledger date by
                    account number with the data being viewed in the format
                    listed above.
         GL0048     Create a Retail Stock Ledger Journal Entry
                    Create a Cost of Sales Journal Entry based on the
                    retail stock ledger data.
                                 MEMBER: DOCGL1                  PAGE:  17
         =======================================================================
                          MAJOR PROGRAM DESCRIPTIONS - cont'd
         =======================================================================
         GLB001     Maintain Budget Files
                    This program will allow users to enter budget amounts
                    into this year's budget file (GLBUDG), next year's budget
                    file (GLBUDGNY) or last year's budget file (GLBUDGLY).
                    An amount may be entered for each period, or one amount
                    may be pro-rated over the total number of periods
                    in the year (12 or 13).
         GLB002     Reset Original Budget Amounts
                    Each account in the budget has an original amount and a
                    modified amount.  This program will replace the original
                    amounts with the modified amounts and will leave the
                    modified amounts intact.  The purpose of this process
                    is to allow the user to be aware of what the original
                    budget for each account was, versus what it currently is.
                    The logical timing of this process would be immediately
                    after acceptance of the original budget.
         GLB004     Move Current Budget to Next Year's Budget
                    This process will copy the current years budget (GLBUDG)
                    into next year's budget (GLBUDGNY).  The purpose of this
                    program is to allow a starting point for next year's budget.
                                   *** END OF REPORT ***
                                 MEMBER: DOCAR1                  PAGE:   1
         ==================================================================
             A C C O U N T S   R E C E I V A B L E   A P P L I C A T I O N
                                O V E R V I E W
         ==================================================================
                           Operates on: IBM AS/400
                  Languages written in: RPG400 & CL
                       First Installed: 1981.
              The Accounts Receivable System can accomodate any number of
         sub-systems by using a series of multi-membered files.  Each program
         is called with a four character company control code used to locate
         the correct member for the accounts receivable files.  The following
         narrative will present a more detailed narrative describing the
         processes that make up the Accounts Receivable Module.
         ==================================================================
                                FEATURES
         ==================================================================
         *  Features an open item system designed for a multi-location
            retail operation.
         *  Accommodates multiple independent Accounts Receivable Systems.
            For example:  Commercial Accounts Receivable
                          Coupons Accounts Receivable
                          Co-Op Advertising Accounts Receivable
         *  Maintains individual customer master files by system.
         *  Allow for separate cut-offs by system.
         *  Allows invoice entry by screen, or by interface with POS system.
         *  Direct, on-line file maintenance.
         *  Provides on-line cash receipts entry with the ability to apply
            payments to the oldest invoices automatically.
         *  Allows customized querey and print fields in the customer
            master file.
         *  Provides full on-line and hard-copy inquiry capability.
         *  Allows for interface with the JDA General Ledger Application.
                                 MEMBER: DOCAR1                  PAGE:   2
         ==================================================================

           ARZCOMM    Commercial Customer Statements
           ARZCOOP    Co-op Advertising Customer Statements
           In addition, all invoice and cash entry is proof listed.

         ==================================================================
                             SUBPROGRAMS USED
         ==================================================================
           ARZ019     A/R Ageing Program
           ARZDAT     Depending on date fields passed, calculates
                      remaining fields.
                      Pass into Program -  DATE1  -  Beginning Date
                                           DATE2  -  Ending Date
                                           DATE3  -  Date Difference
                                           DATE4  -  Months Difference
                                           DATE5  -  Days Difference
           GL0502     Prompt for a memo posting period in Journal Entry
                      batches.
           ARZBLD     Build All the Appropriate Members in A/R
                      Makes sure all of the files are present and creates
                      them if they are not.  Then it adds the members to
                      the files.  This program should be called once for
                      each A/R System Created.

                                 MEMBER: DOCAR1                  PAGE:   5
         ==================================================================
                           DATA BASE OVERVIEW  - cont'd
         ==================================================================
         Master and Table Files:
         -----------------------
          ARZCTL      Company Control File
                      Contains one record for each Company/Accounts
                      Receivable System (i.e. Commercial) being used.
          ARZMST      Customer Master File
                      Contains one record for each customer on file.
                      Each Company/Accounts Receivable System has its
                      own customer master file.
          ARZLOC      Location Table
                      One record for each location.
          ARZSAL      Salesmen Table
                      One record for each salesman.
          ARZAGE      A/R Ageing Date
                      Contains the current ageing date by location.
          OPTIONS     Special Processing Option File
          MENUCTL     System Name File
         Transaction Files:
         ------------------
          ARZOPN      Accounts Receivable Open Item File
                      Contains invoices for the month and invoices
                      for past months on which full payment has not
                      yet been received.  Records remain in the file
                      until purged.  Retains such data as invoice
                      number, invoice date, invoice amount, remaining
                      balance and due date.
          ARZCSH      Accounts Receivable Cash Receipts File
                      Contains monthly receipts.  Records remain in the
                      file until purged.
                                 MEMBER: DOCAR1                  PAGE:   6
         ==================================================================
                           DATA BASE OVERVIEW  - cont'd
         ==================================================================
         History Files:
         --------------
          ARZBAL      Accounts Receivable Balances by Location
                      By location and period/year, this file
                      contains the beginning balance, ending
                      balance, total invoices and total cash receipts.
          Work Files:
          -----------
          WORKFILE    All Purpose Work File.
          ARZBTC      Workstation/Application Batch Control File.
                      Contains the batch control totals for a specific
                      workstation/application and whether or not there
                      are errors present.
          ARZITN      Invoice Entry File
                      Temporary file that contains invoice entry
                      information until posted to ARZOPN.
          ARZCTN      Cash Receipts Entry File
                      Temporary file that contains cash receipts
                      entry information until posted to ARZCSH.
