         =======================================================================
                             MAJOR PROGRAM DESCRIPTIONS
         =======================================================================
         ARZ039     Enter/Update Invoices
                    This program allows you to enter/update the invoice
                    information.  This information includes invoice number,
                    customer number, invoice amount, invoice date, invoice
                    due date and a reference field.
         ARZ012     Edit List of Entered Invoices
                    This program lists the invoices entered and checks the
                    batch control totals entered with the actual batch totals.
                    Both totals are shown on the listing and the user is
                    informed if there is an error present.
         ARZ511     Verify Invoices Entered Don't Already Exist
                    Because entry can be done from many workstations at once,
                    it is possible, because of timing, to enter the same
                    invoice twice.  This program, called in the posting process,
                    reference and description.  Detail information includes
                    checks for the existence of the invoice to be posted.  If
                    it is already there, the invoice is removed from the file
                    (ARZITN) and printed on an exception listing.
         ARZ013     Post Entered Invoices
                    This program writes invoice information from the temporary
                    file (ARZITN) to the open file (ARZOPN) and updates the
                    customer master file (ARZMST) with the date first billed
                    (if applicable) and the date last billed.
         ARZ053     Delete Batch Control Record
                    This program deletes the batch control record for the batch
                    just posted.
         ARZ027     Maintain Open Invoices
                    This program allows the user to change the reference field
                    only on an open invoice.
                                 MEMBER: DOCAR1                  PAGE:  27
         =======================================================================
                          MAJOR PROGRAM DESCRIPTIONS - cont'd
         =======================================================================
         ARZ042     Enter/Update Payments and Adjustments
                    This program allows you to enter/update the payments
                    and adjustments file.  The information entered includes
                    customer number, customer location, transaction code,
                    transaction amount and transaction date.  The user can
                    enter the invoice to apply payment to directly on the
                    first entry screen or go to a second screen of all open
                    invoices and select the invoice to apply payment to.
         ARZ015     Edit List of Entered Payments and Adjustments
                    This program lists the payments and adjustments entered
                    and checks the batch control totals entered with the actual
                    batch totals.  Both totals are shown on the listing and the
                    user is informed if there is an error present.
         ARZ020     Post Entered Payments and Adjustments
                    This program writes the payments and adjustments information
                    from the temporary file (ARZCTN) to the cash file (ARZCSH)
                    and updates the remaining balance in the open invoice file
                    (ARZOPN).  The customer master file (ARZMST) is updated
                    with the last check number, last check date, last check
                    amount and the balance of unapplied and on-account amounts.
         ARZ041     Maintain Payments and Adjustments
                    This program allows the user to adjust the payment amount
                    entered.
                                 MEMBER: DOCAR1                  PAGE:  28
         =======================================================================
                          MAJOR PROGRAM DESCRIPTIONS - cont'd
         =======================================================================
         ARZ017     Change the A/R Ageing Date
                    This program allows the user to change the A/R ageing date
                    for a particular location or for all locations.  This date
                    is used in the ageing reports and in printing the customer
                    statements.
         ARZ028     Print the Customer Statements
                    For every A/R System, there is a customer statement print
                    program defined in the ARZCTL file.  ARZ028 reads the
                    customer master file (ARZMST) and calls this customer
                    statement print program if it is there (CHKOBJ).  If it
                    is not there, ARZ026 is called, the system default customer
                    statement print program.
         ARZ055     Period End Update, Mark All Records with an "X"
                    This program marks all ARZOPN and ARZCSH records for the
                    period with an "X".  It also updates the ending balence
                    in ARZBAL for period just closed and updates the beginning
                    balance for next period.  The total unapplied and total
                    on account field in ARZMST are also updated.
         ARZ510     Creates A/R Journal Entries
                    This program creates journal entries from the A/R system
                    as follows:  For COOP (Co-op Advertising), entries are
                    created for billings and adjustments, for CUPN (Coupons),
                    entries are created for adjustments only and for COMM
                    (Commercial Accounts), no entries are necessary.
         ARZ501     Rebuilds ARZBAL Balances
                    This program reads through the ARZBAL records and rebuilds
                    the beginning and ending balances.  If gaps exist while
                    reading through, a record is created.
                                 MEMBER: DOCAR1                  PAGE:  29
         =======================================================================
                          MAJOR PROGRAM DESCRIPTIONS - cont'd
         =======================================================================
         ARZ024     A/R Purge
                    This program reads through the open file (ARZOPN) and
                    purges all records with an invoice date less than or equal
                    to the purge date specified as long as there is no remaining
                    balance.  Then it reads through the cash receipts file
                    (ARZCSH), if the invoice it is applied to is not found
                    in the open invoice file, the record is deleted.  An audit
                    trail listing of all records purged is also created.
         ARZ507     A/R Balance Record Purge
                    This program reads deletes all ARZBAL records less than or
                    equal to the year and period entered.
                                   *** END OF REPORT ***
                                 MEMBER: DOCMNT1                 PAGE:   1

         =======================================================================
                                    SUBPROGRAMS USED
         =======================================================================
         CHKOBJ     Checks for the existence of an object.  Returns a 'Y'
                    or 'N' return code.
         INV011     Gets next available sku number.
         INV029     Adds a record to INVSTR.
         INV037     Updates or Deletes a record in INVSTR.
         CHKDIG     Calculates the Modulus 10 check digit using a numbering
                    system of character 4.
         CHKDIG1    Checker/Schucks/Kragen's Modulus 10.
         IDATE      Validates a date format and returns a 'Y' in the
                    date is in error.
         ALPHA      Takes a 30 character field and squashes out the blanks
                    and non-alphabetic characters.
         CHKSKU     Validates a sku in INVMST.  This program is used when the
                    INVMST record buffer cannot be overlaid with a second read
                    on a new sku.
         GETUM      Gets the buy/sell unit of measure conversion factors.
         CHG001     Writes the file changes out to the log file (CHGLOG).
         IDEPTS     Retrieves description fields for department, sub-department,
                    class and sub-class.

         =======================================================================
                             MAJOR PROGRAM DESCRIPTIONS
         =======================================================================
         INV001     Sku Master Maintenance
                    This program allows the user to view and update all sku
                    fields or go directly to the 'Page' that contains the
                    information to be updated.  Also, updating of INVSTR
                    and price information is written to a WORKFILE and
                    submitted to batch, rather than on-line. Some important
                    sub-programs get called from here.  CHKDIG1 is a sub-program
                    that calculates the Modulus 10 check digit.  CHKSKU is a
                    sub-program that validates a sku in the sku master (INVMST
                    file when the the INVMST record buffer cannot be overlaid
                    with a second read on a new sku.  ALPHA is the sub-program
                    used to squash out blanks and non-numeric characters from
                    the sku description to create the search code.  CHG001 is
                    a sub-program called to log file changes in the CHGLOG file.
         INV096     Controls Updating of INVSTR Records for Batch Sku Maintenance
                    If this is an Add ('A'), the program calls INV029 to add an
                    INVSTR record.  If this is an Update ('U') or Delete ('D'),
                    it calls INV037 to do the update.  INV029 and INV037 are
                    important sub-programs.
         INV029     Set Up Initial INVSTR Records with Defaults from INVMST
                    This sub-program adds a record to the INVSTR file for the
                    sku/store parameters sent into the program.  If the store
                    is a warehouse, it adds a WHSSKU record.
         INV037     Update/Delete INVSTR Records
                    This sub-program updates or deletes INVSTR records for the
                    sku/store parameters sent to the program.  If the store
                    is a warehouse, it updates or deletes WHSSKU records.
         INV032     Adds All Records for a Store to INVSTR
                    This program adds a record to INVSTR for every sku in
                    INVMST.  Sub-program INV029 is called to perform the add.
                                   *** END OF REPORT ***
                                 MEMBER: DOCIC                   PAGE:   1
         ==================================================================
                       I N V E N T O R Y   C O N T R O L
                               O V E R V I E W
         ==================================================================
                           Operates on: IBM AS/400
                  Languages written in: RPG400 & CL
                       First Installed: 1986.
                                FEATURES
                                ========
         Sku Level Inventory Control
         Tracks movement by Period and YTD for:
                    Sales (Regular & Ad)
                    Returns
                    Receipts
                    Transfers(In & Out)
                    Return to Vendor
                    Adjustment
                    Physical Adjustment
         Stores Sales Data by Week or Period as well, for 2-3 years.
         Allows Manual entry of all transactions or interface from
         other JDA Modules.
         Extensive Searches and Inquiries (see samples)
         Extensive Reports: Weekly Velocity
                            Stock Status
                            Inventory Movement
                            Inventory Evaluation
                            Period End Report
         Extensive Audit Trails.
         Note: The Inventory Control relies on integration with the other
               JDA modules such as Sales Polling & Audit, Transfers,
               Purchase Order/Receiving,  and Cycle Counts to provide
               the transactions for processing. As such, the Inventory
               Control module itself provides little output, but is the
               core module that brings all of the other satellite modules
               together. The design of the system allows for easy
               interface and update of the data files by the fact that there
               is only one interface program, apart from week end, period end,
               and year end processes.
                                 MEMBER: DOCIC                   PAGE:   2
         ==================================================================
                         Inventory Update Documentation              DOCSLS
         ==================================================================
                             Transaction Processing
                               _________________
                               ! File passed to!   File is created
                               ! Inventory     !   manually or as
                               ! Control       !   output from other
                               ! (Invtrn)      !   modules.
                               !_______________!
                                     !
                                     !
                                     !
                             ________V________       Updated Files.
                             ! INVTRN (Work) !       See detailed program
                             ! INVMST        !       logic.
                             ! INVBAL        !
                             ! INVHSTKY      !       Note: IVMMDDYY will not
                             ! INVTOT (Work) !             be cleared by this
                             ! INVDET        !             process.
                             ! INVSUM        !
                             !_______________!
                                 MEMBER: DOCIC                   PAGE:   3
         Program Logic: INEXUPDT
           This program assumes that there is a data file available to be
           processed. The file will be named IVMMDDYY as noted above, will
           be in the same format as file 'INVTRN', and will contain all of
           the sales related inventory transactions from sales polling.
           Step 1. Program will use program 'MBRGET' to obtain the
                   next available random member name e.g. M0005479.
           Step 2. Member will be added to file 'INDWRK'. This file
                   is identical to file 'INVDAT', except it is
                   multi-member.
           Step 3. Data will be copied from file 'INVDAT' to 'INDWRK'.
                   INVDAT contains 1 record representing the current
                   date. That file is maintained daily by program
                   INVD02 which can be accessed off the Calendar
                   Management Menu (MENCAL).
           Step 4. Program INVD02 will be called. Because of an override
                   on the file, the actual file updated will be 'INDWRK',
                   and the member will be the member noted in step 2.
                   If the date displayed agrees with the date the data
                   file IVMMDDYY represents, the operator ends the program.
                   Otherwise the date can be changed.
           Step 5. Program SLS078 will be run which will formulate a parameter
                   that will contain the date represented in file 'INDWRK'
                   e.g. IV011587. This will be the same as the name of the
                   file to be processed.
           Step 6. A CHKOBJ will be done to ensure that the file is present.
                   If not, a message will be displayed and the program will
                   end. If it is, the program 'INEXUPJQ' will be submitted
                   with the name of the file, and the member name formed in
                   Step 1 passed as parameters.
           Step 7. Program INEXUPJQ begins.
                                 MEMBER: DOCIC                   PAGE:   4
           Step 8. Members, using the member name passed in Step 6 are added
                   to the following files.
                   INVTRN     (Inventory Transaction File)
                   INVTRNL1   (Logical on INVTRN)
                   INVTOT     (Total records used in INVDET & INVSUM update)
                   LIMITS     (Limits file)
           Step 9. Appropriate overrides are done.
           Step 10. Program INV108 runs. This program will copy the records
                    from IVMMDDYY to INVTRN. This step may be modified to
                    simply utilize the file IVMMDDYY, but for conversion
                    periods it should be left as is.
                    Note: The transaction date in the file passed to the
                    program will not be used. The date represented in the
                    file name will be used instead.
           Step 11. A detailed list of the transactions is done. This should
                    be kept during conversion, and removed later, once the
                    system is performing properly.
           Step 12. Program INV038 Runs. This is the major inventory update
                    program. The files updated are as follows.
                    INVTRN  : (Through view INVTRNL1) as each record
                              is read, it is flagged, to prevent re-posting
                              on a re-run. Field ITFLAG is updated with an
                              'X'. Any record encountered with an 'X' in this
                              field will be bypassed. Note that the program
                              logic allows for re-processing a file and having
                              all of the records bypassed, but still creating
                              the summary records in INVTOT. The reasoning is
                              that the logic to back out a partially processed
                              file in terms of the files INVBAL,INVMST, &
                              INVHSTKY, will be difficult, but since INVTOT
                              is a result of processing this file only, it can
                              be rebuilt. As well, INVTOT is updated at total
                              time, while the others are updated at detail time.
                                 MEMBER: DOCIC                   PAGE:   5
                    INVMST  : NOTE - if the transaction record is for an item
                              that does not exist in the item master, it will
                              be bypassed. The Item master is updated only
                              with 'CHAIN ON HAND' and 'CHAIN ON ORDER'.
                    INVBAL  : This file is updated with all inventory and
                              sales related data, including Week to date,
                              Period to date, and Year to date data.
                              Note that this is the major update program to
                              this file. The only other updates other than
                              conversion are related to order quantities and
                              week/period/year end processing.
                    INVHSTKY: For any transaction that is for a date which is
                              not within the current week, two records will be
                              written to this file. At week end, INVHSTKY
                              will be merged with the sales history file
                              INVHST and consequently, the sales will be
                              correct for each week. This could result in
                              the PTD sales for the current period for an
                              item not being the same as that represented
                              in the detailed history file (INVHST), but
                              only for the current period, and only for those
                              items that have sales posted to a prior period
                              in the current period. Attempting to post sales
                              for a future period will result in them being
                              posted to the current period.
                              Feature: This feature allows sales for last
                                       week to be processed this week, and
                                       the correct results to be maintained.
                    INVTOT  : This file contains the '16' records for file
                              INVSUM, and the '64' records for file INVDET.
                              See record layouts for details.
                                 MEMBER: DOCIC                   PAGE:   6
           Step 13. Program ISUMUPDT Gets Submitted. See separate
                    program logic. (ISUMUPDT updates INVSUM &
                    INVDET) Note that the program is submitted
                    so that it may run concurrently with the
                    balance of the steps.
           Step 14: Program INV109 is called, to see if weekend, period end
                    or year end is to be done.
           Step 15: If week end is to be done, program INVWEND is called.
                    Otherwise the program ends.
         *** Week end Processing ***
         *** NOTE: For storing sales by week, record 1070 in the options file
                   must indicate Weekly sales. See Options file documentation
                   in member 'DOCSYS' for details on checking, or look on
                   MENU MENCTL. This is critical.
           Step 16: A file called 'INVHSTNW' (INVHST new) is built by
                    the CRTDUPOBJ command, the same as INVHST. The file at
                    this point is empty.
           Step 17: If there is a file present called 'INVHSTLP' present, it
                    is deleted.
           Step 18: The file 'INVHST' is renamed 'INVHSTLP'.
           Step 19: A blank 'INVHST' file is created.
           Step 20: Program INV073 is run. Fields relating to weekly data in
                    'INVBAL' are updated, and records representing the weekly
                    sales history are written to the file 'INVHSTNW' created
                    in step 16 above, if sales are to be stored by week.
                                 MEMBER: DOCIC                   PAGE:   7
           Step 21: If sales is recorded by period, and this is not period end,
                    the program goes to STEP 26.
           Step 22: If sales is recorded by period, and this is period end, do
                    program INV053, and then do the merging step.
                    Program INV053 fields ralating to period data in 'INVBAL'
                    are updated, and records representing the weekly sales
                    history are written to the file 'INVHSTNW' created
                    in step 16 above, if sales are to be stored by period.
           Step 23: If sales is recorded by week, which will be the case if
                    the program reaches this step, do the merging step.
         *** The merging step ***
           Step 24: The following files are merged to create a new 'INVHST'
                    INVHSTKY: History adjustments created in Step 12 above,
                              and any keyed adjustments.
                              Keyed adjustments that only affect 'INVHST'
                              can be entered using program INV060. This
                              program does not appear on any menu and was
                              not intended for day to day use. It can be
                              used as a patch process only, or as an aid
                              in conversion.
                    INVHSTNW: Which now contains new data for the period or
                              week, depending on how sales is stored.
                    INVHSTLP: File containing all data to end of the last
                              complete week/period.
                    Also, file 'INVBAL' is updated with the relative record
                    numbers in 'INVHST' as follows:
                          a) 1st record in 'INVHST' for this item.
                          b) 1st record in 'INVHST' for this item for this
                             year.
                                 MEMBER: DOCIC                   PAGE:   8
           Step 25: INVHSTLP, INVHSTNW, and INVHSTKY are cleared.
           Step 26: If this is the year end, the program 'INVYEND' is called.
           Step 27: Year end processing consists of:
                    a) Program INV074. This program will reset all YTD fields
                       in 'INVBAL', and initialize all 'beginning of year'
                       fields.
           Step 28: End of sales processing.
                                 MEMBER: DOCIC                   PAGE:   9
         Program Logic: ISUMUPDT
         This program is submitted by the daily inventory external update
         program (INEXUPDT). Its' purpose is to process the file 'INVTOT' and
         update the sales summary files 'INVSUM' & 'INVDET'.
           Step 1. An override is done to the proper member in the file
                   'INVTOT' as created in program 'INEXUPDT', and likewise,
                   to the proper 'LIMITS' file.
           Step 2: Program INV089 is called.
                   Based in the information in records 1100 - 1165 in the
                   options file, the appropriate records in 'INVSUM' and
                   'INVDET' are created, or updated (if already present).
                   The records in 'INVTOT' are deleted as processed.
                   The lowest (earliest) date encountered is saved during the
                   processing, and at program end, a record is written to the
                   'LIMITS' file to be used in the next step.
           Step 3. Using the 'LIMITS' record created in the preceding step,
                   program 'INV102' processes the file 'INVSUM', updating the
                   year to date fields for each record that is equal to or
                   later than the earliest date encountered in the preceding
                   step. This feature allows sales data from previous days to
                   be processed, and to the have the YTD fields in records
                   represent any subsequent days to the earliest day to have
                   their YTD fields updated accordingly, without processing
                   the entire file.
                                 MEMBER: DOCIC                   PAGE:  10
         Re-classification of Store/Day File: (INVSUM)
         Program Logic: INV091CL
         This program (INV091CL) must be run if a store is assigned to
         a different district, or a district to a different region. The
         program can be accessed from the MIS manager's menu (MENMIS).
         The file 'INVSUM' cannot be used when the program is running.
         The steps are as follows:
           Step 1: Add a member to 'INVTOT' & 'LIMITS'
           Step 2: Run program 'INV091CL' creating the '16' records in
                   'INVTOT', picking up the proper region and district for
                   each store from the Store File (TBLSTR).
           Step 3: Clear the file 'INVSUM'
           Step 4: Re-create 'INVSUM' from the '16' records in 'INVTOT' using
                   the program 'INV089'. A record will be added to 'LIMITS'
                   but will not be used.
           Step 5: Remove the members from 'INVTOT' & 'LIMITS'
           Step 6: Recalculate the YTD fields in each record in 'INVSUM' by
                   running program 'INV102CL'. Program 'INV102CL' can,
                   incidentally, be run any time the YTD fields have been
                   damaged.
                                 MEMBER: DOCIC                   PAGE:  11
         Reclass.of Store/Day/Dept/Sub-dept/Class/Sub-class File (INVDET).
         Not yet complete, however, steps as follows:
         CLRPFM INVDET
         CALL INV117 - Creates INVDET '64' Records from INVHST.
         CALL ?????? - Copy INVDET '64' records into INVTOT
         CALL INV089 - Re-create all records in INVDET.
                                 MEMBER: DOCIC                   PAGE:  12
         Other Considerations:
         Manual Update of Sales Data:
         The only other update of sales data is done through the
         'Manual Update of Inventory Transactions', (program INV039CL)
         which can be accessed from the INVENTORY CONTROL MENU (MENINV).
         Individual item sales records can be entered and processed, and the
         same basic steps as 'INEXUPDT' will occur, with the exception of
         the week/period/year end logic.
         Week end/Period end/Year end processing:
         In order for any of these processes to be triggered, a sales run,
         (program INEXUPDT) must be run for the last day of each week, period,
         and year, whether data is available or not.
                                   *** END OF REPORT ***
                                 MEMBER: DOCAV                   PAGE:   1

            - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -
                 P R I C I N G   L O G I C   D E S C R I P T I O N
            - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -
         The following describes the price change logic contained in the
         price management programs.
            1. MAKING PRICE CHANGES.
                RECORDS IN 'IPRICE' CAN BE AFFECTED IN THREE WAYS:
                   A/ INV001 SKU MAINTENANCE PROGRAM - 000 RECORD ONLY.
                   B/ AD PLANNING MODULE, BASED ON EFFECTIVE DATE
                      FOR 000 RECORD, OR ONE STORE ONLY
                   C/ PRICE CHANGE MODULE, ALLOWS CHANGES TO 000 RECORD
                      OR ONE STORE.
             THIS PROGRAM (PRC001) WILL PASS THROUGH THE ENTIRE PRICE
             FILE ON A NIGHTLY BASIS, AND DECIDE WHETHER A PRICE
             ENTERED IN ANY OF THE ABOVE MANNERS IS NOW EFFECTIVE, BASED
             ON TODAYS DATE. IF IT IS, A 'P' IS PLACED IN THE FIELD
             'POSFLG'. IF, AT THE END OF DEALING WITH EACH SKU, THE
             PRICE CHANGE FLAG IS ON, IT MEANS THAT THERE NEEDS TO BE
             A PRICE CHANGE/ADDITION SENT TO THE POS EQUIPMENT.
             THE PROGRAM WILL UPDATE THE FILE 'INVSTR' WITH A 'P'
             IN IT'S RECORD.
             BY STORE:
             PROGRAM 'DTS010' WILL, FOR PROMPTED STORE, PASS THROUGH
             THE FILE 'INVSTR', LOOKING ONLY AT RECORDS THAT HAVE
             A 'P', PLACED THERE BY THIS PROGRAM, AND WILL CREATE THE
             APPROPRIATE RECORDS IN DTSOUTF AND DTSCMDF TO SEND TO
             THE POS DEVICE.  DEPENDING ON WHETHER OR NOT THIS SKU
             IS ON THE POS FOR THIS STORE (ISNEW = ' '), THE PROGRAM
             WILL DETERMINE ADD OR CHANGE.
             AFTER THE RECORD IS CREATED TO GO TO POS, THE INVSTR
             FILE HAS THE 'P' REMOVED AND ADDED RECORDS HAVE
             THE FIELD 'ISNEW' SET TO 'Y'.
                                 MEMBER: DOCAV                   PAGE:   4
            ----------------------------------------------------------
                        PRICING ROUTINE
              1.IF REGULAR EFFECTIVE DATE TODAY OR EARLIER,
                    A. MOVE NEXT REG PRICE TO CUR POS PRICE
                    B. MOVE CUR  REG PRICE TO LAST REG PRICE
                    C. MOVE NEXT REG EFF. DATE TO DATE LAST CHANGED
                    D. MOVE NEXT REG PRICE TO CUR REG PRICE
                    E. MAKE NEXT REG PRICE 0
                    F. MAKE REG EFFECTIVE DATE 0
                    G. MOVE 'P' TO POSFLG
                    H. DO PERMANANT MARKDOWN/MARKUP ROUTINE
              2. IF TODAY IS EQUAL TO OR GREATER THAN EFFECTIVE FROM DATE
                 AND LESS THAN OR EQUAL TO EFFECTIVE TO DATE,
                 AND NEXT AD PRICE IS NOT EQUAL TO CUR POS PRICE,
                    A. MOVE NEXT AD  PRICE TO CUR POS PRICE
                    B. MOVE 'P' TO POSFLG
                    C. MOVE 'A' TO AD ACTIVE FLAG
                    D. SET MARKUP/DOWN $'S TO ZERO
              3. IF AD PRICE IS GT 0, AND TODAY IS OR GT ADD
                 EFFECTIVE TO (DEAL END) DATE,
                    A. MOVE CUR REG  PRICE TO CUR POS PRICE
                    B. MOVE ADD EFF DATE TO AD DATE LAST CHANGED
                    C. MOVE NEXT AD PRICE TO LAST ADD PRICE
                    D. MOVE 'P' TO POSFLG
                    D. MOVE ' ' TO POSFLG ADD ACTIVE FLAG
                    E. MOVE 0'S TO NEXT AD PRICE, EFFECTIVE DATES
                       AND AD NUMBER.
                    F. DO TEMPORARY MARKDOWN/MARKUP ROUTINE
                    G. SET MARKUP/DOWN $'S TO ZERO
            * NOTE: SALES AUDIT SYSTEM WILL CHECK FOR ACTIVE
                    AD'S AND ACCUMULATE MRKUP/DOWN $'S
                                 MEMBER: DOCAV                   PAGE:   5
              -----------------------------------------------------
                           EXPLANATION OF POLLING
              -----------------------------------------------------
         The following describes a typical POLLING routine utilizing the
         modules affecting price management and inventory control.
         This routine utlizes SNC Sierra Controllers and DTS 521 Cash Registers.
         (Installed at Courtesy Home Center, Chicago, Illinois)
         The routine described is typical of polling requirements
                STEP 1. ANY JOBS THAT ARE TO BE RUN AFTER POLLING
                        PROCESSING ARE TO BE PLACED IN THE JOB Q
                        'NIGHTQ'. THIS JOBQ WILL BE ON HOLD
                        DURING THE DAY. THE ARRIVAL OF EACH OF
                        THESE JOBS IS DETERMINED BY THAT JOB'S
                        INITIATION.
                STEP 2. JOB 'POLLSTRT' IS RUN ONE OF TWO WAYS.
                        A/ AT THE END OF EACH NIGHTS POLLING
                           PROCESS, JOB POLLEND WILL SUBMIT THE
                           'POLLSTRT' JOB. IN OTHER WORDS, IT IS
                           SELF PERPUTATING UNTIL THE MACHINE IS
                           TURNED OFF, OR THE JOB 'POLLWAIT' IS
                           OTHERWISE CANCELLED.
                        B/ IF, FOR SOME REASON, THE POLLING
                           PROCESS MUST BE STARTED MANUALLY, THE
                           'POLLSTRT' JOB CAN BE INITIATED OFF OF
                           THE POLLING MENU (MENPOL). IT SHOULD
                           ONLY BE STARTED IF POLLING IS NOT
                           ALREADY RUNNING IN THE SUBSYSTEM
                           'QBATCH' OR IN THE JOBQ 'POLLQ'.
                STEP 3. JOB 'POLLSTRT' SUBMITS JOB 'POLLWAIT'
                        TO JOBQ 'POLLQ.'
                                 MEMBER: DOCAV                   PAGE:   6
                STEP 4. JOB 'POLLWAIT' FIRST WAITS UNTIL AT LEAST
                        6 PM IN THE EVENING. IF IT IS AFTER
                        6 PM, IT WILL RUN PROGRAM POLL02, WHICH
                        DETERMINES THE CURRENT DATE FROM THE
                        POLLING DATE FILE 'POLLDATE'. THE ACCURACY
                        OF THIS FILE IS ABSOLUTELY CRITICAL. THEN
                        PROGRAM POLL03 STARTS. THIS PROGRAM WILL
                        LOOK EVERY TEN MINUTES TO SEE WHETHER
                        ONE OF THE FOLLOWING HAS HAPPENED:
                         A/ THE TIME TO START POLLING FOR THIS
                            PARTICULAR DAY HAS COME.
                                 OR
                         B/ SOMEONE HAS REQUESTED THAT POLLING
                            START IMMEDIATELY.
                        THE OPTIONS FILE IS USED IN THE ABOVE
                        PROCESS AS FOLLOWS
                        RECORD
                        NUMBER  CONTROLLING DATA
                         1050   SHOULD POLLING START EARLY ?
                         1051   TIME TO START POLLING - SUNDAY
                         1052   TIME TO START POLLING - MONDAY
                         1053   TIME TO START POLLING - TUEDAY
                         1054   TIME TO START POLLING - WEDNESDAY
                         1055   TIME TO START POLLING - THURSDAY
                         1056   TIME TO START POLLING - FRIDAY
                         1057   TIME TO START POLLING - SATURDAY
                                 MEMBER: DOCAV                   PAGE:   7
                STEP 5. WHEN JOB 'POLLWAIT' HAS FINISHED WAITING,
                        IT SENDS A MESSAGE TO QSYSOPR STATING THAT
                        'POLLING PROGRAM HAS AWAKENED.'
                        IT THEN SUBMITS TO JOBQ 'POLLQ' THE
                        FOLLOWING TWO JOBS.
                        POLLRUN - RUN THE ACTUAL POLLING PROCESSES.
                        POLLHOLD - SUBMIT A JOB TO JOBQ 'NIGHTQ'
                                   WHICH PUTS JOBQ 'NIGHTQ' ON
                                   HOLD. THIS WILL BE THE LAST
                                   JOB IN 'NIGHTQ'. THE POLLING
                                   PROCESS WILL, AS A LAST STEP,
                                   RELEASE THE 'NIGHTQ' SO THAT
                                   THE SUBMITTED DESCRIBED IN
                                   STEP ONE ABOVE WILL RUN. THE
                                   LAST STEP, OF COURSE, IS TO
                                   PUT THE JOBQ ON HOLD SO THAT
                                   ANYTHING PUT IN THERE TOMMORROW
                                   WILL BE ON HOLD UNTIL NIGHT TIME
               STEP 6. THE 'POLLRUN' PROCESS.
               STEP 6A.FIRST STEP IN POLLING RUN IS TO WALK THROUGH
                       THE PRICE FILE AND DETERMINE WHETHER OR
                       NOT PRICES HAVE BECOME EFFECTIVE. IF THE
                       SKU REQUIRES A PRICE CHANGE, BASED ON THE
                       EFFECTIVE DATES (REGULAR OR AD), THEN THE
                       RECORD IS UPDATED WITH THE NEW POS PRICE,
                       AND THE APPROPRIATE RECORDS ARE UPDATED
                       IN THE 'INVSTR' FILE, INDICATING THAT A
                       PRICE CHANGE IS 'PENDING' FOR THIS SKU IN
                       THIS STORE.
               STEP 6B.THE NEXT STEP IS TO DETERMINE, IN PROGRAM
                       'POLL05', WHICH STORES IN THE STORE TABLE
                       FILE (TBLSTR) ARE: 1) STORES (NOT HEAD
                       OFFICES OR WAREHOUSES) AND 2) HAVE POS
                       ACTIVE. FOR EACH OF THESE, A JOB CALLED
                       SETUPXXX (XXX IS STORE NUMBER) IS SUBMITTED
                       TO THE JOBQ 'POLLQ'
               STEP 6C.USING THE SAME PROGRAM (POLL05) AND THE
                       SAME LOGIC AS 6B, A JOB CALLED DIALXXX IS
                       SET UP IN 'POLLQ' FOR EACH ACTIVE STORE.
               STEP 6D.A JOB CALLED 'POLLEND' IS SUBMITTED TO
                       'POLLQ'. THIS JOB WILL RUN AFTER THE
                       SETUP, DIAL, AND PROC RUNS ARE COMPLETE.
                       THIS JOB IS DESCRIBED BELOW.
                                 MEMBER: DOCAV                   PAGE:   8
                CONTENTS OF POLLING JOB QUEUE  (POLLQ)
                AFTER 'POLLRUN', THE JOB QUEUE LOOKS THUSLY:
                      SETUP001
                      SETUP002
                      SETUP003 ...ETC.
                      DIAL001
                      DIAL002
                      DIAL003  ...ETC.
                      POLLEND
               SETUPXXX: CREATES THE FOLLOWING FILES
                         DTSCMDF - COMMANDS TO BE SENT TO THE
                                   SIERRA REQUESTING:
                                   A) RECEIVE PRICE CHANGES
                                   B) SEND REPORTS:
                                       018 - ITEM MOVEMENT
                                       085 - CASHIER REPORT
                         DTSINPF - PRICE CHANGES/ADDITIONS/DELETES
                         DTSRESP - BLANK FILE TO CONTAIN COMMUNIC-
                                   ATIONS RESPONSES.
                         DTSOUTF - BLANK FILE TO CONTAIN DATA
                                   RECEIVED FROM SIERRA
                         WITH THE EXECPTION OF 'DTSRESP', THESE
                         FILES ARE 'MEMBERED' BY STORE (S001 E.G.)
               DIALXXX: WILL RETRIEVE THE TELEPHONE NUMBER FOR
                        THIS STORE FROM THE STORE TABLE FILE
                        (TBLSTR) AND CHANGE THE CONTROL UNIT TO
                        USE IT. IT WILL THEN RUN PROGRAM
                        'DTSTALK', WHICH WILL ATTEMPT UP TO
                        THREE TIMES TO ESTABLISH COMMUNICATIONS
                        WITH THE STORE LEVEL SIERRA.
                        IF COMMUNICATIONS IS NOT ESTABLISHED, OR
                        IF IT IS ESTABLISHED BUT THE TRANSMISSION
                        IS IMCOMPLETE, NO FURTHER PROCESSING IS
                        DONE FOR THIS STORE. ERROR WARNING
                        MESSAGES ARE SENT TO THE SYSTEM OPERATOR.
                        IF IT IS COMPLETE, DTSTALK WILL SUBMIT
                        JOB 'PROCXXX'
               PROCXXX: THIS JOB WILL PROCESS THE INFORMATION
                        RECEIVED FROM THE SIERRA.
                                 MEMBER: DOCAV                   PAGE:   9
               NOTE: EVEN THOUGH PROGRAM 'POLLEND' WAS SUBMITTED
                     BEFORE 'PROCXXX',IT WAS GIVEN THE LOWEST
                     PRIORITY, SO IT WILL RUN DEAD LAST.
               POLLEND : THIS PROGRAM WILL DO THE FOLLOWING
                         1) INCREMENT THE DATE IN 'POLLDATE'
                            BY ONE DAY.
                         2) CLEAR ANY JOBS FROM 'BBATCH'
                         3) SUBMIT 'BACKNIGHT' - THE BACKUP
                            TO DISK.
                         4) WAIT UNTIL AT LEAST 1 A.M.
                         5) SUBMIT POLLSTRT
                PROCESS NOW BEGINS ALL OVER AGAIN.
                                   *** END OF REPORT ***
                                 MEMBER: DOCSINQ                 PAGE:   1

               S A L E S   P E R F O R M A N C E   A P P L I C A T I O N
                                O V E R V I E W
         ==================================================================
                           Operates on: IBM AS/400
                  Languages written in: RPG400 & CL
                       First Installed: 1986.
                                FEATURES
                                ========
         Allows screen display of Sales, Sales by Customer, Sales by
         Manhour, Customers by Manhour, Customers, and Gross Margin.
         Lowest level of display is Store totals for each day.
         Allows rolling up into weeks or periods.
         Allows comparison of same day (or week or period) to same
         day last year.
         Allows all of the displays to show individual stores, or
         summary into region or district level.
         Ability to Graphically represent relevant columns of data.
                                 MEMBER: DOCSINQ                 PAGE:   2
                             DATA FILE OVERVIEW
                             ==================
                                   (INVSUM)
         Daily Summary Sales File: Contains daily information for
                                   each store including Sales, Costs,
                                   Customer Counts, and Manhours.
                                   (TBLSTR,TBLDST,TBLREG,INVCAL,INVDAT)
                      Table Files: Including Store File, District File,
                                   Region File, and Calendar Files.
                                   (INVTOT)
                   Interface File: Work File used as interface into Sales
                                   Summary file from other systems, or
                                   from manual entry.
                                 MEMBER: DOCSINQ                 PAGE:   3

         ==================================================================
                     SALES PROCESSING APPLICATION OVERVIEW
         ==================================================================
                           Operates on: IBM AS/400
                  Languages written in: RPG400 & CL
                       First Installed: 1986
         The Sales Processing Module is designed to capture historical
         records of daily sales initiated at the store level.
         ==================================================================
                                  FEATURES
         ==================================================================
         * Performs automated week end, period end and year end update.
         * Permits inquiry at multiple levels including SKU, store, chain,
           region, district, department or subdepartment, class or subclass.
         * Users may determine at what level to maintain the above-mentioned
           information via simple control files.
         * Inquiries are designed to illustrate data in graph form for selected
           criteria.
         * Special menu options permit the submission of all available reports
           via a single execution, rather than multiple requests.
                                 MEMBER: DOCSLS1                 PAGE:   2

         ==================================================================
                           DATA BASE OVERVIEW - cont'd
         ==================================================================
         Master Files:
         INVMST    Item Master File
                    Sku master file with one record for every sku.
         INVBAL    Inventory Balance Master
                    Keeps inventory balance and movement data by store/sku.
         INVSUM    Inventory & Sales Summary
                    Daily inventory and sales summary by store.
         INVDET    Inventory & Sales Detail
                    Weekly inventory and sales summary by store and sub-class.
         INVHST    Inventory History Last Period
                    Weekly inventory and sales detail by store and sku number.
         Table Files:
         OPTIONS   The Applications Options File
         TBLSTR    Table of Valid Stores
         INVCAL    Calendar File
         INVDAT    Current Date
         Transaction Files:
         IV000000  External Sales Transaction File
         INVHSTKY  Inventory History File, Previous Period's Transactions
                    Weekly inventory and sales detail by store and sku
                    number for the previous period.
         POMDTL    P.O. Detail File
                    Contains the detail information by sku for a purchase order.
         Work Files:
         INVTRN    Inventory Transactions
         INVMBR    Totals From the Sales Run
         LIMITS    General Purpose Limits File
         WRKDET    Working Copy of INVDET (inventory & sales detail)
         INVHSTNW  Inventory History Current Period
         INVHSTLP  Inventory History Last Period

         =======================================================================
                             MAJOR PROGRAM DESCRIPTIONS
         =======================================================================
         INV108     Copy File to INVTRN
                    This program converts an external file to INVTRN and
                    picks up the department, sub-department, class and
                    sub-class from INVMST.
         INV038     Major Inventory Update Program
                    This program updates the sales figures and on-hand
                    quantites in the store INVBAL records and creates the
                    INVTOT records which are later used to update INVDET
                    and INVSUM.
         INV089     Update the Sales Summary File
                    This program updates the sales summary file (INVSUM)
                    from the INVTOT file created in INV038.
         INV102     Rebuilds the YTD Fields in INVSUM
                    This is a rebuild program that reads through INVSUM
                    and accumulates the YTD figures.  If they are different
                    from what is presently in the file, the file is updated.
         INV073     Updates the Week Fields in INVBAL
                    This program rolls current weeks sales in week 1, week 1
                    into week 2, etc. up to week 8 and recalculates the average
                    weekly sales.  The current week figures are then zeroed
                    out for the new week.
                                 MEMBER: DOCSLS1                 PAGE:  13
         =======================================================================
                             MAJOR PROGRAM DESCRIPTIONS
         =======================================================================
         INV053     Period End Update of History and INVBAL
                    This program updates the inventory history file at period
                    end and updates (resets) the period-to-date figures in
                    INVBAL.
         INV054     Period End Update of Inventory History File
                    This program creates the inventory history file at period
                    end from INVHSTLP, INVHSTNW and INVHSTKY (created in INV038).
         INV074     Updates Year End Fields in INVBAL
                    This program updates the on-hand beginning of year (IBHBOY)
                    and on-hand beginning of period (IBHBOP) fields in INVBAL   .
                    and zeroes out the current fields for next year.            .
                                   *** END OF REPORT ***

                                 MEMBER: DOCCOST                 PAGE:   2
         =======================================================================
                       J D A   S O F T W A R E   S E R V I C E S
                                  PRODUCT COSTING
         =======================================================================
         The system handles two alternate types of costing methods:
                         1) Current Cost
                         2) Average Cost
         The determination of which method to use can be controlled at the
         store or warehouse (location) level by maintaining an indicator in
         the store table file (TBLSTR). This is managed by using the Store
         Table Maintenance program (TBL005).
         ** WARNING **
         Once the indicator is changed, all transactions created after that
         point will use the new method, so the timing of making a change to
         the costing method used is very critical.
         On the Store Table File, one of three options can be entered:
                  'C' = Use the current cost method.
                  'A' = Use the average cost method.
                  'W' = For stores only, use the associated warehouse's
                        method, be it 'C' or 'A'.
         ** WARNING **
         If you mistakenly tell the system to use the associated warehouse's
         method for a warehouse, the system will produce unpredictable results.
         Determination of which cost to use:
         Steps:  1) Access the store table file to get the method type (C,A,W)
                 2) If the type is 'W' access the associated stores' store table
                    file record to get it's method type, and use it in the
                    following steps.
                 3) If the type is blank, default the type to 'C'.
                 4) If the type is 'C':
                          Note that the Current Cost is stored in the files
                          INVCST and INVMST in the BUY Unit of Measure
                          and this must be multiplied by the Buy/Sell conversion
                          factor to get the Sell Unit Cost.
                           a) Access the SKU/Store Cost record in the file
                              INVCST to get the CURRENT COST (ICUCST)
                           b) If no record was found in a), access the
                              item master file (INVMST) and use the
                              current cost (ICUCST)
                           c) Multiply the current cost by the buy/sell
                              conversion factor (IUMCV2) to get the Transaction
                              Unit Cost.
                                 MEMBER: DOCCOST                 PAGE:   3
                           d) Put the Transaction Unit Cost on the Inventory
                              Transaction Record (INVTRN).
                 5) If the type is 'A':
                           a) Use the Stores Average Cost (IBAVGC) for this
                              Sku and put it on the Inventory Transaction
                              Record (INVTRN). This field is stored in the
                              file INVBAL and is calculated as noted below in
                              program INV038. Note that the 'store' in the case
                              of the average cost method being 'W' would be the
                              store's associated warehouse.
         Where costs are used:
         The system uses only one major program to do the inventory updating
         process (INV038). All transactions fed into this program are
         contained in the file INVTRN. When these records are created, the
         proper unit (Sell unit) cost must be written to the record.
         The JDA system will put the cost amount on the record in the
         following programs/sub-systems.
               Program    Description
               =======    ===================================================
               INV039     Manual entry of transactions.
               TRF027     Creation of 'Transfer Out' records.
               GETCST     Sub-Program to get Transaction Costs
                    (to be continued)
         Maintaining Costs:
         1) Current Costs:
         Current costs can be maintained at the chain level or by individual
         location through the item maintenance program (INV001) and/or by
         using the programs in the COST CHANGE ADMINISTRATION menu. These
         are a series of programs that allow upcoming cost changes to be
         recorded in the system and then downloaded, or put into affect, as
         of a certain date.
         2) Average Costs:
         The average cost calculation is performed in program INV038 and is
         calculated as follows:
         Only the following transaction types are considered:
                       31   Regular Receipt
                       32   AD      Receipt
                       33   Receipt Adjustment
                       41   Transfer In
                       46   Transfer Adjustment
                       61   Adjustment
                       83   Initial File Load
                                 MEMBER: DOCCOST                 PAGE:   4
         Steps:  1) Take the Average cost before the transaction and multiply
                    it by the quantity on hand before the transaction. Save the
                    result.
                 2) Take the unit cost on the transaction and multiply it by the
                    transaction quantity and save the result.
                 3) Add the results of step one and step two.
                 4) Calculate the new quantity on hand after processing the
                    transaction.
                 5) Divide the result of step three by the new quantity on
                    hand to get the new average cost.
                 6) Store the new average cost in the file INVBAL in the
                    field IBAVGC.
         Example:
                  Had 100 on hand at $6.00 each.
                  Received 50 more at unit cost of 6.50 each.
                  (100 * 6.00) + (50 * 6.50)
                  --------------------------   = $6.167 (New average cost)
                         (100 + 50)
         * Note: If the new quantity on hand is zero, the average cost will
                 be zero ]
         INQUIRY:
         ========
         Costs for a sku by location can be displayed by using the inquiry
         program INV141CL, which is also on the Inventory Inquiries Menu.
                                   *** END OF REPORT ***

                                 MEMBER: DOCPOM1                 PAGE:   4
         ==================================================================
                                 SUBPROGRAMS USED
         ==================================================================
         CST012     Gets override cost for SKU using the INVCST file.
         DATE02     Depending on date fields passed in, calculates the
                    remaining date fields.
         GETPAL     Retrieves the allowance/discoutn rates from the P.O.
                    allowance file (POMALW).
         GETSTR     Gets default store number for workstation.
         GETUM      Gets the buy/sell unit of measure conversion factors.
         IDATE      Checks validity of given date.
         IDEPTS     Retrieves description fields for department, sub-department,
                    class and sub-class.
         PRC003     Used to get appropriate retail price for SKU.  Chaining to
                    IPRICE does not obtain the correct price.
         POMBAL     Rebuild on order quantity in INVBAL.
         POMUPD     Re-crank the totals in the purchase order header record.
         POMUPR     Re-crank the totals in the purchase order receiving
                    header record.
         POMSTS     Get the purchase order status that corresponds to the
                    option code.
         POM002     Get the next available purchase order number.
         POM023     Get the next available purchase order recevier number.

         =======================================================================
                             MAJOR PROGRAM DESCRIPTIONS
         =======================================================================
         WHS010     Pick Merchandise For Transfers  (Step 1)
                    This program is the first step in the Pick Transfers
                    Process.  It is executed by selecting and submitting
                    transfers to be picked.
                    It's purpose is to create Work Move records (WRKMOV) and
                    commit the inventory from the SKU's primary.  If there is
                    not enough inventory in the SKU's primary then it calls
                    a letdown program (WHS087) which will try and letdown
                    from the overstock locations into primary so that when
                    program control is passed back to WHS010, the inventory
                    may be committed. Sku cost is obtained at this time and
                    updated in transfer detail so that if the shipping
                    mannifest is pre-printed the SKU will have a cost.
         WHS050     Pick Merchandise For Transfers  (Step 2)
                    This program is the second step in the Pick Transfers
                    Process.  It's purpose is to read the Work Move records
                    written during the first step (WHS010) by Zone, Pick Sort
                    Sequence, and From Location and create the Move Header
                    and Move Detail records for both the Pick Transfer Moves
                    and the Letdown To Primary Moves. If the zones being
                    picked from are conveyable then it also computes the
                    number of bar code labels required at picking time.
                    Output from this program is a summary of the number of
                    picking stops, total cube etc. for the picking job.
         WHS087     Letdown From Overstock To Primary
                    The letdown program is executed from one of two processes:
                    the Picking Process or the Manual Letdown Process.
                    It's function is to find the closest overstock locations
                    with which it can letdown to primary to satisfy a quantity
                    required. If two locations are of the same distance from
                    primary then it chooses the location with the earliest
                    receiving date. The process creates Work Move records
                    which are input to WHS050 program which creates the
                    Move Header and Move Detail records with a letdown type.
         WHS014     Enter/Approve Move Documents
                    This is a generic program used to approve the quantities
                    that have been moved.   It is used for all move types in
                    the warehouse.  The approval processing (WHS049) can be
                    executed at this time or from a "batch" approval submit
                    (WHS070) at a later time.  It is executed by selecting
                    a move document number to approve at which time it displays
                    the move's associated details for user modification.
         WHS072     Enter Picking/Letdown Move Exceptions
                                 MEMBER: DOCPOM1                 PAGE:  19
                    This program allows the user to enter those details
                    that had move exceptions and execute the approval process
                    of these move documents at this time or, as with the Enter/
                    Approve Move Documents program, the move documents may be
                    submitted for approval processing at a later time using a
                    "batch" submit .  The exceptions are entered using
                    the information on the pick ticket: Move #, Sequence #,
                    and SKU.
         WHS070     Batch Approve Move Documents
                    The purpose of this program is to batch submit moves
                    to the aproval process (WHS049).  All the move documents
                    for a warehouse, to location or specific transfer are
                    dislayed allowing the user to select the move document
                    for approval processing and enter the clerk's initials
                    at the same time.
         WHS049     Approve Move Documents
                    The Move Document Approval program has a number of
                    responsibilities depending on the move document type.
                    It's major function (common to all moves) is that it updates
                    the location cubes and the committed and pending of locations
                    with any exception quantities not moved and then generates
                    an Inventory Transaction record (INVTRN) which will be
                    input the to major inventory update program INV038.  This
                    program will deplete the quantity from the from location
                    and increase the quantity of the to location.  The function
                    of the program INV038 in the Warehouse Inventory Control
                    module will be discussed further later on in this narrative.
                    A more detailed description of how WHS049 functions for
                    different move types can be found under their respective
                    headings.
         INV038     Major Inventory Update Program
                    This program plays a crucial role in Warehouse
                    Inventory Control.  It is responsible for actually updating
                    the quantities of warehouse locations (except in the case of
                    Internal Warehouse Moves) and updating the warehouse
                    perpetual inventory.  It relieves committed and pending,
                    quantity allocated for a SKU, computes location cube, and
                    records shipment history in IWHSHP ( Warehouse Shipments
                    History) file.  An option exists allowing the user to select
                    a listing of all INVTRN transactions which have been created
                    for the process (INV041 program).
         WHS037     Internal Warehouse Moves
                    This program allows direct manipulation of SKU quantities
                    in locations.  A move is performed "On-Line", depleting the
                    from location with the quantity and adding it to the to
                    location.  The quantity allowed to be moved cannot be
                    greater than what is physically onhand less any already
                    committed inventory.  This process does not create  Move
                    records.
                                 MEMBER: DOCPOM1                 PAGE:  20
         WHS095     Manual Letdowns
                    Manual Letdown is initiated on the Letdown Menu and
                    is used to create "moves" from overstock to primary
                    locations when the quantities onhand in the primary have
                    decreased below a specified replenishment percentage.
                    All primarys that meet this criteria are replenished if
                    possible by creating Move Records from the overstock
                    locations to the primary locations.
                                   *** END OF REPORT ***
                                 MEMBER: DOCREC                  PAGE:   1
         ======================================================================
                   R E C E I V I N G   M O D U L E   O V E R V I E W
         ======================================================================
                                       FEATURES
                                       ========
         Allows for on-line receipt of merchandise against a displayed P.O.
         Provides for control-numbered Receiving Authorizations (KEYREC) to
         be attached to a P.O.
         Provides for exception based receiving:
                       - Receive Complete, or;
                       - Enter exception quantities or items only
         Provides ability to post multiple receivings against a single P.O.
         Allows for the receipt of overshipped merchandise.
         Allows receiving against ordered or transferred merchandise.
         Provides for Vendor returns with reason code at time of receiving.
         Allows for backordered merchandise.
         Tracks and reports on Vendor Delivery Performance.
         Tracks and Reports on Vendor Returns.
         Produces tickets and shelf labels upon receipt of goods.
         Allows for multiple ticket and label formats.
         Allows for partial receipts against a P.O.
         Allows for item search by both Vendors' Part Number and Description.
         Creates transfers from pre- or post-distributed item receipts.
                          STANDARD REPORTS AND INQUIRIES
                          ==============================
         Screen Inquiries: Open P.O. Inquiry
                           Open Receiving Authorization Inquiry
         Standard Reports: Daily Receiving Journal
                           Receiving Exception Report
                           Receiving Confirmation Report
                                   *** END OF REPORT ***
                                 MEMBER: DOCTRF1                 PAGE:   1
         ==================================================================
                       WAREHOUSE/STORE TRANSFER MODULE
         ==================================================================
                           Operates on: IBM AS/400
                  Languages written in: RPG400 & CL
                       First Installed: 1987
         The Transfer module tracks the movement of merchandise between any
         two locations.  It can be initiated as a regular or supplemental
         store order, a store return to warehouse, and transfers of inventory
         between two stores or two warehouses.
                                 MEMBER: DOCTRF1                 PAGE:   2
         ==================================================================
                                  FEATURES
         ==================================================================
         * Detailed inquiry and reporting of transfers at each stage of the
           inventory movement process.  Statuses include work, approved(open),
           shipped, and received.  (An additional status is used for warehouse
           transfers while they are in the picking process.)
         * Allows transfers to be generated centrally or at the store level.
           Limits access to transfers based on location (i.e. only allows a
           store or warehouse to work with transfers for which they are either
           the sending or receiving location).
         * Option to automatically receive shipped transfers into a store
           (i.e. assume a transfer is received complete) or alternatively to
           receive the transfers through the transfer receiving process.
         * Provides several approaches to building orders centrally for
           groups of stores both out of (distributions) and in to (returns)
           warehouses.
                                 MEMBER: DOCTRF1                 PAGE:   3

                    defined in TBLSTR.
         PRC003     Used to get appropriate retail price for SKU.  Chaining to
                    IPRICE does not obtain the correct price.
         MENU16     Used to get values from the OPTIONS file in a control
                    language program.

                                 MEMBER: DOCTRF1                 PAGE:   6
         Table Files:
         INVDAT     Current Date File
                    Contains current date information such as current period,
                    week of period, week of year, current year, current day
                    and a literal description of the date.
         TBLZON     Warehouse Zone File
                    Table of valid zones by store with such information as
                    zone name, zone type, and whether it is the primary zone,
                    etc.
         WHSCLK     Warehouse Clerk File
                    Table of valid clerks for the a store and their name.
         INVUMR     Inventory Unit Of Measure
                    Table of valid units of measure with a description and
                    a sell/buy factor.
         OPTIONS    Special Processing Option File
         TBLSTR     Table of Valid Stores
         MENUCTL    Menu Control File Name
         Master Files:
         INVMST     Inventory Master File
                    Contains such sku information as description, department,
                    sub-department, class, sub-class and cost information.
         INVBAL     Inventory Balance Files
                    Keeps inventory balance and movement data by store/sku.
         IWHSTR     Warehouse Shipment Transactions
                    Warehouse transaction file that contains the transaction
                    code, year and week of transaction and transaction quantity.
         IWHSHP     Warehouse Shipment History
                    Warehouse/Sku weekly shipments containing current and
                    previous shipping quantities.
         Transaction Files:
         TRFHDR     Transfer Header File
                    Contains one record for every transfer batch with the
                    batch name, status, from and to location and other header
                    information.
         TRFDTL     Transfer Detail File
                    Contains sku level transaction information like quantity
                    requested, shipped, received and allocated.
         TRFEXC     Transfer Interface Exception File
                    Contains sku ordered, exception code and the replaced or
                    substituted sku.
         TRFINT     Transfer Interface File
                    Contains store, sku, requested quantity, retail out and
                    minimum order quantity.
         TRFSSH     Transfer Sku Set Header
                    Contains one record for every sku set with the sku set
                    name and description.
         TRFSSD     Transfer Sku Set Detail
                    Sku set detail information, sku set name, sku and sequence
                    number.
                                 MEMBER: DOCTRF1                 PAGE:   7
         Work Files:
         WRKMOV     Work Move File
                    Work file for building move transactions.
         LIMITS     General Purpose Limits File
         INVTRN     Inventory Transaction File
         WORKFILE   Workfile (Limits type) File
