         =======================================================================
                             MAJOR PROGRAM DESCRIPTIONS
         =======================================================================
         TRF046     Allocate Transfers
                    The program is used to allocate and de-allocate inventory
                    in the WHSSKU file.  The process will allocate up to the
                    amount requested depending on the amount available to be
                    allocated. The amount available to be allocated is computed
                    as follows: On hand (from INVBAL file) less quantity
                    already allocated less quantity reserved.
         TRF033     Cancel A Transfer
                    The cancel process can only be initiated for a transfer
                    that has either a Work status or an Allocated status. The
                    process then de-allocates the inventory (if the transfer was
                    allocated) and deletes the transfer and it's associated
                    detail records.  Sub-program TRF046 actually updates the
                    allocated in the warehouse sku master (WHSSKU).
         TRF027     Ship Transfers
                    The program is used to ship transfers either from a store or
                    a warehouse. If the transfer is being shipped to a store
                    and the option to auto receive is on, then this process will
                    also receive the inventory into the to location. Inventory
                    transactions are written to update onhand in the INV038
                    program. Markout and scratchouts are computed by sku and
                    update the IWHSTR file.  The retail-out is obtained for the
                    transfer detail record and if the transfer is being shipped
                    from a store, the unit cost is also retrieved at this time.
         TRF067     Over/Short Transfer Adjustment
                    The over/short adjustment program is used to adjust
                    inventory onhand balances for any locations that shipped
                    over or short. If the adjustment involves a warehouse then
                    the adjustment must be made "from" the warehouse and the
                    quantities being adjusted will be in the Shipping Adjustment
                    Slot defined in WHSDFT file. The respective to location's
                    onhand will also be updated and these updates take place
                    in the INV038 program.
         TRF030     Receive Transfers
                    The receiving process writes out inventory transactions to
                    update onhand and any adjustments needed if quantity shipped
                    is not equal to quantity received. Retail-in is acquired if
                    it is not already on the transfer detail.
         TRF041     Purge Transfers
                                 MEMBER: DOCTRF1                 PAGE:  19
                    This program purges those transfers that have a status of
                    Complete and a receive date less than or equal to the
                    date entered on the Purge Transfers screen. An audit trail
                    of purged records is printed.
         INV038     Major Inventory Update Program
                    This program plays a crucial role in Warehouse
                    Inventory Control.  It is responsible for actually updating
                    the quantities of warehouse locations (except in the case of
                    Internal Warehouse Moves) and updating the warehouse
                    perpetual inventory.  Sub-program TRF046 is called to update
                    the allocated quantities in the warehouse location records
                    (WHSSKU).  It relieves committed and pending, quantity
                    allocated for a SKU, computes location cube, and records
                    shipment history in IWHSHP ( Warehouse Shipments History)
                    file.  Sub-program WHS029 is called if a warehouse location
                    sku (WHSLSK) record needs to be created.  An option exists
                    allowing the user to select a listing of all INVTRN
                    transactions which have been created for the process.
                    (INV041 is the listing program.)
                                   *** END OF REPORT ***
                                 MEMBER: DOCWHS1                 PAGE:   1
         ==================================================================
                       WAREHOUSE INVENTORY CONTROL OVERVIEW
         ==================================================================
                           Operates on: IBM AS/400
                  Languages written in: RPG400 & CL
                       First Installed: 1987
         The Warehouse Inventory Control module is the sum of smaller
         sophisticated processes which all have basically one common
         purpose: to track inventory movement in a warehouse.  It is the
         intention of this narrative to present an overview of how inventory
         movement is tracked.
                                 MEMBER: DOCWHS1                 PAGE:   2
         ==================================================================
                                  FEATURES
         ==================================================================
         * Highly parameterized with respect to picking, putaway, and letdown
           logic.  System organizes the warehouse/DC into zones.  Each zone
           can be managed differently within the system by varying patameter
           selection by zone.
         * Provides up to date information on sku locations in warehouse,
           quantities onhand, quantities committed to be moved, and
           quantities pending into locations.
         * Performs single primary location picking which incorporates letdown
           logic from overstock locations.
         * Performs "random" picking for items with no designated primary
           location.  Picks from oldest location first.
         * Suggests full pallet and partial pallet putaway locations using
           location capacities and warehouse coordinate system.
         * Provides ability for internal warehouse moves from one location
           to another.
         * Features detailed reporting and inquiry of quantities being moved
           in all stages of the inventory movement process.
         * Has ability to tie in original transfer documents with the moves
           being performed in the warehouse.
                                 MEMBER: DOCWHS1                 PAGE:   3
         ==================================================================

                    defined in TBLSTR.

                    IPRICE does not obtain the correct price.

         Table Files:
         INVDAT     Current Date File
                    Contains current date information such as current period,
                    week of period, week of year, current year, current day
                    and a literal description of the date.
         TBLZON     Warehouse Zone File
                    Table of valid zones by store with such information as
                    zone name, zone type, and whether it is the primary zone,
                    etc.
         WHSCLK     Warehouse Clerk File
                    Table of valid clerks for the a store and their name.
         INVUMR     Inventory Unit Of Measure
                    Table of valid units of measure with a description and
                    a sell/buy factor.
         OPTIONS    Special Processing Option File
         TBLSTR     Table of Valid Stores
         MENUCTL    Menu Control File Name
         Master Files:
         INVMST     Inventory Master File
                    Contains such sku information as description, department,
                    sub-department, class, sub-class and cost information.
         INVBAL     Inventory Balance Files
                    Keeps inventory balance and movement data by store/sku.
         WHSSKU     Warehouse Sku File
                    Warehouse sku master that keeps allocated and reserved
                    quantities by warehouse/sku.
         WHSLOC     Warehouse Location File
                    Warehouse slot location file that keeps such slot
                    information as cube capacity, cube used, cube available
                    and the slots corresponding zone.
         WHSLSK     Warehouse Location Sku File
                    Keeps on-hand, committed and pending for a warehouse/slot/sku.
         WHSDFT     Warehouse Default File
                    Keeps defaults by warehouse such as default receiving and
                    shipping zones and default receiving and shipping slots.
         IWHSTR     Warehouse Shipment Transactions
                    Warehouse transaction file that contains the transaction
                    code, year and week of transaction and transaction quantity.
         IWHSHP     Warehouse Shipment History
                    Warehouse/Sku weekly shipments containing current and
                    previous shipping quantities.
         Transaction Files:
         WHSMVH     Warehouse Move Header File
                    Contains one record for every move document number.
         WHSMVD     Warehouse Move Detail File
                    Keeps move detail information such as quantity requested
                    and quantity moved.
         WHSMVX     Warehouse Move Cross Reference
                    Item/Location move cross reference containing the move
                                 MEMBER: DOCWHS1                 PAGE:   7
                    status and source document number.
         WHSMVS     Warehouse Move Document To Store Picking Information
                    Contains to location, total labels and total cube.
         TRFHDR     Transfer Header File
                    Contains one record for every transfer batch with the
                    batch name, status, from and to location and other header
                    information.
         TRFDTL     Transfer Detail File
                    Contains sku level transaction information like quantity
                    requested, shipped, received and allocated.
         Work Files:
         WRKMOV     Work Move File
                    Work file for building move transactions.
         LIMITS     General Purpose Limits File
         INVTRN     Inventory Transaction File
         WORKFILE   Workfile (Limits type) File

         =======================================================================
                             MAJOR PROGRAM DESCRIPTIONS
         =======================================================================
         WHS037     Internal Warehouse Moves
                    This program allows direct manipulation of SKU quantities
                    in locations.  A move is performed "On-Line", depleting the
                    from location with the quantity and adding it to the to
                    location.  If a Warehouse Location Sku (WHSLSK) record needs
                    to be added, a call to sub-program WHS029 accomplishes this
                    task.  The quantity allowed to be moved cannot be greater
                    than what is physically onhand less any already committed
                    inventory.  This process does not create Move records.
         WHS050     Pick Merchandise For Transfers  (Step 2)
                    This program is the second step in the Pick Transfers
                    Process.  It's purpose is to read the Work Move records
                    written during the first step (WHS010) by Zone, Pick Sort
                    Sequence, and From Location and create the Move Header
                    and Move Detail records for both the Pick Transfer Moves
                    and the Letdown To Primary Moves. If the zones being
                    picked from are conveyable then it also computes the
                    number of bar code labels required at picking time.
                    Output from this program is a summary of the number of
                    picking stops, total cube etc. for the picking job.
         WHS087     Letdown From Overstock To Primary
                    The letdown program is executed from one of two processes:
                    the Picking Process or the Manual Letdown Process.
                    It's function is to find the closest overstock locations
                    with which it can letdown to primary to satisfy a quantity
                    required. If two locations are of the same distance from
                    primary then it chooses the location with the earliest
                    receiving date. The process creates Work Move records
                    which are input to WHS050 program which creates the
                    Move Header and Move Detail records with a letdown type.
         WHS095     Manual Letdowns
                    Manual Letdown is initiated on the Letdown Menu and
                    is used to create "moves" from overstock to primary
                    locations when the quantities onhand in the primary have
                    decreased below a specified replenishment percentage.
                    All primarys that meet this criteria are replenished, if
                    possible, by creating Move Records from the overstock
                    locations to the primary locations.  This program
                    program decides if the criteria are met, if Move Records
                    are to be created, it calls sub-program WHS087 to do that.
                                 MEMBER: DOCWHS1                 PAGE:  15
         =======================================================================
                           MAJOR PROGRAM DESCRIPTIONS - cont'd
         =======================================================================
         WHS014     Enter/Approve Move Documents
                    This is a generic program used to approve the quantities
                    that have been moved.   It is used for all move types in
                    the warehouse.  The approval processing (WHS049) can be
                    executed at this time or from a "batch" approval submit
                    (WHS070) at a later time.  It is executed by selecting
                    a move document number to approve at which time it displays
                    the move's associated details for user modification.
         WHS071     Change Status of Move Header to Processed
                    This program changes the status of the move header record
                    to 'PROCESSED'.  Also, if the status is already 'PROCESSED'
                    when this program is called, the WORKFILE record is deleted.
         WHS049     Approve Move Documents
                    The Move Document Approval program has a number of
                    responsibilities depending on the move document type.
                    It's major function (common to all moves) is that it updates
                    the location cubes and the committed and pending of locations
                    with any exception quantities not moved and then generates
                    an Inventory Transaction record (INVTRN) which will be
                    input to the major inventory update program INV038.  This
                    program will deplete the quantity for the from location
                    and increase the quantity of the to location. If a warehouse
                    location sku (WHSLSK) record needs to be created, the
                    sub-program WHS029 is called to perform this task.  Move
                    Header records are updated as "processed', Move Cross
                    Reference records (WHSMVX) are updated as "processed" for
                    pick moves, and occupied flags are relieved for any
                    quantities "marked-out".
         INV038     Major Inventory Update Program
                    This program plays a crucial role in Warehouse
                    Inventory Control.  It is responsible for actually updating
                    the quantities of warehouse locations (except in the case of
                    Internal Warehouse Moves) and updating the warehouse
                    perpetual inventory.  Sub-program TRF046 is called to update
                    the allocated quantities in the warehouse location records
                    (WHSSKU).  It relieves committed and pending, quantity
                    allocated for a SKU, computes location cube, and records
                    shipment history in IWHSHP ( Warehouse Shipments History)
                    file.  Sub-program WHS029 is called if a warehouse location
                    sku (WHSLSK) record needs to be created.  An option exists
                    allowing the user to select a listing of all INVTRN
                    transactions which have been created for the process.
                    (INV041 is the listing program.)
                                   *** END OF REPORT ***
                                 MEMBER: DOCPUT1                 PAGE:   1
         ==================================================================
               WAREHOUES INVENTORY CONTROL - PUTAWAY PROCESSING
         ==================================================================
                           Operates on: IBM AS/400
                  Languages written in: RPG400 & CL
                       First Installed: 1987
         The Putaway Process is used to move merchandise off the receiving
         dock and into primary and overstock locations.
                                 MEMBER: DOCPUT1                 PAGE:   2
         ==================================================================
                                  FEATURES
         ==================================================================
         * Provides up to date information on sku locations in warehouse,
           quantities onhand, quantities committed to be moved, and
           quantities pending into locations.
         * Suggests full pallet and partial pallet putaway locations using
           location capacities and warehouse coordinate system.
         * Features detailed reporting and inquiry of quantities being moved
           in all stages of the inventory movement process.
                                 MEMBER: DOCPUT1                 PAGE:   3

                   .TBLSTR        .

                 ...............    ----->¦  WHS130  ¦      . WORKFILE:MBR .
                 ...............          ¦ Approve  ¦-----> .WHSMVH        .
                  . INVDAT       .  ----->¦ Putaway  ¦      .              .
                   .OPTIONS       ./      +----------+     ................
                  . MENUCTL      .             ¦
                 ...............               ¦
                                               V
                                          Process Moves
                                 MEMBER: DOCPUT1                 PAGE:  11
         =======================================================================
                             MAJOR PROGRAM DESCRIPTIONS
         =======================================================================
         WHS045     Putaway To Primary And Overstock Locations
                    The purpose of the program is to putaway merchandise
                    from the warehouse receiving dock.  It is executed by
                    selecting zones and skus for putaway in the Warehouse
                    Receiving Menu. If new warehouse location sku (WHSLSK)
                    records need to be added, sub-program WHS029 is called
                    to perform this task. Based on the SKU's volume and the cube
                    of a full pallet putaway, locations are suggested in the
                    following manner:
          *****
                    Rules of Putaway:
                         A)  All eaches (partial pallets) must be putaway
                             in a group after the step to fill primary.
                             In other words, if 10 eaches remain you cannot
                             put 5 in one slot and 5 in the other.
                         B)  Replenishment percentage is checked only on
                             the primary to ensure that the putaway has exceeded
                             it's replenishment percentage (this is to avoid a
                             letdown occuring during a manual letdown possibly
                             sometime later in the day).
                         C)  All cube capacities are augmented with a putaway
                             rounding percentage (if entered) to provide a
                             small fill buffer in cases where the cube of the
                             inventory being putaway is just slightly greater
                             than the capacity of the slot.
                         D)  A zone must be specified as replenish = "Y" for a
                             slot to be considered in the putaway process.
                         E)  An empty overstock location is defined as
                             occupied flag = "0" - Non Occupied and in a
                             non-primary zone.
                         F)  If a zone on an empty overstock is defined as
                             consolidate = "N", then the slot must contain
                             no other inventory before it is suggested (used
                             cube equal to zero).
                             Note: To consolidate a zone implies that if a
                                   sku already exists in a location, put
                                   inventory away there before looking at
                                   empty overstock locations.
                                 MEMBER: DOCPUT1                 PAGE:  12
         =======================================================================
                          MAJOR PROGRAM DESCRIPTIONS - cont'd
         =======================================================================
                         G)  All slots are selected for validation based on
                             their proximity to the primary location using
                             their coordinates. The selection process is
                             based on a look ahead, look behind sequence.
                             For example, if the process looks ahead and
                             finds a slot that is not valid, it's next
                             step is to look behind and validate. If that slot
                             is deemed a valid putaway location then after
                             creating a putaway record the next step is to
                             look ahead again.
          *****
                         1)  The first attempt is to put away inventory to
                             it's primary slot. The process trys to putaway
                             any partial pallets (eaches).  If the primary
                             is still not full then the process attempts
                             to putaway full pallets.  A percentage of pending
                             + onhand merchandise vs total capacity is taken
                             and if the percentage derived is not greater than
                             the replenishment percentage then a full pallet is
                             broken and the primary is filled to it's capacity
                             (capacity in this instance is the primary slot
                             capacity only, disregarding putaway rounding
                             percent).
                         2)  The second step is to putaway inventory to
                             consolidate zones.  This is done by looking only
                             at Location/Sku records with consolidated,
                             non-primary zones (with replenish = "Y" and
                             attempting to putaway the inventory (always full
                             pallets first, then partial pallets).
                         3)  The third step is to putaway remaining inventory
                             to empty overstock locations. The process, at this
                             step, looks for non-occupied (occupied flag = 0)
                             locations in non-primary zones (with replenish = "Y).
                             Starting at primary, it looks ahead and behind for
                             the closest empty overstock locations. If a
                             non-occupied location is found in a zone which is
                             defined as a non-consolidate zone and the location
                             already contains inventory, then it is bypassed.
                                 MEMBER: DOCPUT1                 PAGE:  13
         =======================================================================
                          MAJOR PROGRAM DESCRIPTIONS - cont'd
         =======================================================================
                         4)  If after steps one, two, and three have been
                             completed and there still is inventory left to
                             putaway then the putaway from location is suggested
                             as the to location and a putaway records is written
                             so that a new location may be entered at
                             Enter/Approve Putaway time.
          *****
         WHS129     Enter Putaway Exceptions
                    This program allows the user to enter those details
                    that had move exceptions and execute the approval process
                    of these move documents at this time or, as with the Enter/
                    Approve Move Documents program, the move documents may be
                    submitted for approval processing at a later time using a
                    "batch" submit .  The exceptions are entered using
                    the information on the putaway document: Move #, Sequence #,
                    and SKU.  Either the quantity putaway or the location
                    that it was putaway to can be modified.
         WHS130     Batch Approve Putaway Documents
                    The purpose of this program is to batch submit putaway moves
                    to the aproval process (WHS049).  All putaway documents
                    for a warehouse are displayed allowing the user to select
                    the move document(s) for approval processing and enter
                    the clerk's initials at the same time.
                                   *** END OF REPORT ***
                                 MEMBER: DOCPCK1                 PAGE:   1
         ==================================================================
          WAREHOUSE INVENTORY CONTROL - PICKING AND APPROVE PICKS PROCESS
         ==================================================================
                           Operates on: IBM AS/400
                  Languages written in: RPG400 & CL
                       First Installed: 1987
         An important process in Warehouse Inventory Control is the Picking
         Process.  This process can be defined as the movement of inventory
         from primary locations to the shipping dock as a result of a transfer
         out of the warehouse or a return to vendor.
                                 MEMBER: DOCPCK1                 PAGE:   2
         ==================================================================
                                  FEATURES
         ==================================================================
         * Provides up to date information on sku locations in warehouse,
           quantities onhand, quantities committed to be moved, and
           quantities pending into locations.
         * Performs single primary location picking which incorporates letdown
           logic from overstock locations.
                                 MEMBER: DOCPCK1                 PAGE:   3

         Table Files:
         INVDAT     Current Date File
                    Contains current date information such as current period,
                    week of period, week of year, current year, current day
                    and a literal description of the date.
         TBLZON     Warehouse Zone File
                    Table of valid zones by store with such information as
                                 MEMBER: DOCPCK1                 PAGE:   6
                    zone name, zone type, and whether it is the primary zone,
                    etc.
         WHSCLK     Warehouse Clerk File
                    Table of valid clerks for the a store and their name.
         INVUMR     Inventory Unit Of Measure
                    Table of valid units of measure with a description and
                    a sell/buy factor.
         OPTIONS    Special Processing Option File
         TBLSTR     Table of Valid Stores
         MENUCTL    Menu Control File Name
         Master Files:
         INVMST     Inventory Master File
                    Contains such sku information as description, department,
                    sub-department, class, sub-class and cost information.
         INVBAL     Inventory Balance Files
                    Keeps inventory balance and movement data by store/sku.
         WHSSKU     Warehouse Sku File
                    Warehouse sku master that keeps allocated and reserved
                    quantities by warehouse/sku.
         WHSLOC     Warehouse Location File
                    Warehouse slot location file that keeps such slot
                    information as cube capacity, cube used, cube available
                    and the slots corresponding zone.
         WHSLSK     Warehouse Location Sku File
                    Keeps on-hand, committed and pending for a warehouse/slot/sku.
         WHSDFT     Warehouse Default File
                    Keeps defaults by warehouse such as default receiving and
                    shipping zones and default receiving and shipping slots.
         IWHSTR     Warehouse Shipment Transactions
                    Warehouse transaction file that contains the transaction
                    code, year and week of transaction and transaction quantity.
         Transaction Files:
         WHSMVH     Warehouse Move Header File
                    Contains one record for every move document number.
         WHSMVD     Warehouse Move Detail File
                    Keeps move detail information such as quantity requested
                    and quantity moved.
         WHSMVX     Warehouse Move Cross Reference
                    Item/Location move cross reference containing the move
                    status and source document number.
         WHSMVS     Warehouse Move Document To Store Picking Information
                    Contains to location, total labels and total cube.
         TRFHDR     Transfer Header File
                    Contains one record for every transfer batch with the
                    batch name, status, from and to location and other header
                    information.
         TRFDTL     Transfer Detail File
                    Contains sku level transaction information like quantity
                    requested, shipped, received and allocated.
         Work Files:
         WRKMOV     Work Move File
                    Work file for building move transactions.

         =======================================================================
                             MAJOR PROGRAM DESCRIPTIONS
         =======================================================================
         WHS010     Pick Merchandise For Transfers  (Step 1)
                    This program is the first step in the Pick Transfers
                    Process.  It is executed by selecting and submitting
                    transfers to be picked.
                    It's purpose is to create Work Move records (WRKMOV) and
                    commit the inventory from the SKU's primary.  Two major
                    sub-programs are involved in this process.  WHS087 creates
                    the WRKMOV records and TRF046 updates the allocated in
                    the warehouse sku master (WHSSKU).  If there is not enough
                    inventory in the SKU's primary then it calls a letdown
                    program (sub-program WHS087) which will try and letdown
                    from the overstock locations into primary so that when
                    program control is passed back to WHS010, the inventory
                    may be committed.
                    There are basically two types of zones to pick from
                    which determine the way the Move Documents are created:
                    by Zone/Location/Store or by Store/Zone/Location.
                    The WRKSRT field plays an important role in sorting
                    the WRKMOV records properly (for details see program
                    source documentation). If a pick quantity exception
                    occurs, the quantity is relieved immediately
                    from the SKU allocation.  A report is produced
                    listing all pick exceptions that have occurred.
                    All quantities that are picked are "pended" to the
                    shipping dock and location cube used is updated for
                    the from location.
                    If the entire transfer was "scratched" (all details could
                    not be picked) then the transfer status is updated to
                    "picked" at this time.
         WHS050     Pick Merchandise For Transfers  (Step 2)
                    This program is the second step in the Pick Transfers
                    Process.  It's purpose is to read the Work Move records
                    written during the first step (WHS010) by Zone, Work Sort,
                    Pick Sequence, and From Location and create the Move Header
                    and Move Detail records for both the Pick Transfer Moves
                    and the Letdown To Primary Moves. A letdown document
                    is unique for a warehouse, move type (letdown), move status
                    (open), from zone, and to zone.  Letdown move details are
                    created with a sequence number of 50,000 or higher so that
                    they can be resorted from 1 by from or to zone at letdown
                    document printing time.
                    A pick document is unique for every from zone or to store
                    and from zone for transfers submitted in a job.
                    The transfer status is updated to "picked" here and a
                    Move Cross Reference record (WHSMVX) is written for every
                    new move document - transfer combination.
         WHS072     Enter Picking/Letdown Move Exceptions
                                 MEMBER: DOCPCK1                 PAGE:  13
                    This program allows the user to enter those details
                    that had move exceptions and execute the approval process
                    of these move documents at this time or, as with the Enter/
                    Approve Move Documents program, the move documents may be
                    submitted for approval processing at a later time using a
                    "batch" submit .  The exceptions are entered using
                    the information on the pick ticket: Move #, Sequence #,
                    and SKU.
         WHS070     Batch Approve Move Documents
                    The purpose of this program is to batch submit moves
                    to the aproval process (WHS049).  All the move documents
                    for a warehouse, to location or specific transfer are
                    dislayed allowing the user to select the move document
                    for approval processing and enter the clerk's initials.
                                   *** END OF REPORT ***

         ==================================================================
                       WAREHOUSE PHYSICAL INVENTORY MODULE
         ==================================================================
                           Operates on: IBM AS/400
                  Languages written in: RPG400 & CL
                       First Installed: 1987
         The Warehouse Physical Inventory module  is the sum of smaller
         sophisticated processes which all have basically one common
         purpose: to track inventory movement in a warehouse.    It is the
         intention of this narrative to present an overview of how inventory
         movement is tracked.  A more detailed narrative describing the
         processes that make up this module can be found in the following
         pages.
         ==================================================================
                                  FEATURES
         ==================================================================
         * Provides ability to do a Full Physical Inventory or Cycle Counts by
           Zone or Vendor.
         * Allows for Pre-Printing of Count Sheets before freezing the On-Hand
           amounts.
         * Provides option to include empty slots to allow for entry of
           supplemental counts.
                                 MEMBER: DOCWPI1                 PAGE:   2
         ==================================================================
                          STANDARD REPORTS & INQUIRIES
         ==================================================================
         Screen Inquiries:
         WPI005    Physical Inventory Batch Search
         WPI012    Physical Inventroy Batch/Section Search
         WPI039    Physical Inventory Count Inquiry
         Standard Reports:
         WPI016    Count Variance Report
         WPI034    Count Variance SKU Summary
         WPI026    Slot/Section Report
         WPI028    Batch/Section Report
         WPI032    Zone/Section Report
         WHS104    Item Cost Listing
         ==================================================================
                                 SUBPROGRAMS USED
         ==================================================================
         GETCST     Gets appropriate cost for SKU depending on Costing Method
                    defined in TBLSTR.
         PRC003     Used to get appropriate retail price for SKU.  Chaining to
                    IPRICE does not obtain the correct price.
         GETSTR     Gets default store number for workstation.
         CORCST     Used to get appropriate core cost of a SKU.
         IDEPTS     Retrieves description fields for department, sub-department,
                    class and sub-class.

         =======================================================================
                             MAJOR PROGRAM DESCRIPTIONS
         =======================================================================
         WPI003     Warehouse Physical Inventory Selection by Zone
                    This program allows the user to select (or un-select)
                    the zones to be included in the Cycle Count by Zone.
                    Zones selected are written to a WORKFILE.
         WPI041     Full Warehouse Physical Inventory Selection
                    This program selects ALL zones for a Full Warehouse
                    Inventory are writes them to a WORKFILE.
         WPI042     Warehouse Physical Inventory Selection by Vendor
                    This program allows the user to select (or un-select)
                    the vendors to be included in the Cycle Count by Vendor.
                    Vendors selected are written to a WORKFILE.
         WPI004     Builds Detail Records for Full Physicals and Cycle Counts
                    by Zone
                    This program reads a WORKFILE of zones and selects all
                    slot/sku's for those zones and builds the detail (WHSPID)
                    records.
         WPI043     Builds Detail Records for Cycle Counts by Vendor
                    This program reads a WORKFILE of vendors and selects all
                    slot/sku's for those vendors and builds the detail (WHSPID)
                    records.
         WPI007     Edit List
                    This program provides a hard copy edit list so the user can
                    see what slot/sku's were selected.
                                 MEMBER: DOCWPI1                 PAGE:  17
         =======================================================================
                          MAJOR PROGRAM DESCRIPTIONS - cont'd
         =======================================================================
         WPI035     Create Detail Records for Empty Slots
                    The purpose of the program is to create physical inventory
                    detail (WHSPID) records for empty slots.  It is executed
                    within the pre-print count sheets job stream and within
                    the release physical inventory batch job stream.  It is
                    used to ensure that all slots within each zone have been
                    included so that the user may later enter supplemental
                    counts.
         WPI036     Create Page and Sequence numbers
                    This program creates page numbers and sequence numbers
                    in the physical inventory detail (WHSPID) file.  The detail
                    records are read in store, batch name, section # and slot
                    sequence.  The sequence number is incremented by one for
                    each detail record and the page number is incremented by
                    one for each fifteen detail records of when the section
                    number changes,  Physical inventory cross reference (WHSPIX)
                    records are also created in this program when a new page
                    number is encountered.
         WPI011     Count Sheets
                    This program prints count sheets by Zone/Section/Page#.
                    The count sheet includes 15 slot/sku's per page with room
                    to enter two separate counts and the initials of the person
                    who did the count.
         WPI009     Release Physical Inventory Batch
                    The purpose of this program is to update the physical
                    inventory detail (WHSPID) records with the current on hand
                    balance, which is known as the "freeze" count.  The detail
                    records also get updated with the current unit cost and
                    the current unit retail prices.  The page and sequence
                    numbers are also created, if not previously done so through
                    the pre-print job stream.  The sequence number is incre-
                    mented by one for each detail record and the page number
                    is incremented by one for every fifteen detail records or
                    when the section number changes.  When a new page number is
                    encountered, a physical inventory cross reference (WHSPIX)
                    record is created.
                                 MEMBER: DOCWPI1                 PAGE:  18
         =======================================================================
                          MAJOR PROGRAM DESCRIPTIONS - cont'd
         =======================================================================
         WPI013     Enter/Update Counts
                    This program is used for then entering and/of updating of
                    physical inventory counts.  The user is prompted for section
                    number and page number, the program then retrieves all of
                    the inventory items on the specified page of the count
                    sheets and will allow the user to enter counts.  For slots
                    which were found to be empty by program WPI035, the program
                    allows entering of the SKU number which resides in that
                    slot.  If a new SKU has been entered, the program will
                    update its detail record with the "freeze" count, current
                    cost and current retail price.
         WPI029     Enter Re-Counts
                    The purpose of this program is to allow entry of re-counted
                    slots.  The user must enter for each count the section
                    number, page number, sequence number, SKU number and new
                    count.  Editing is performed to ensure validity of the data
                    entered.
         WPI030     Enter Supplemental Counts
                    This program allows the user to enter slot/SKU combina-
                    tions which have not yet been defined to the system.
         WPI018     Process Counts
                    This program writes inventory transaction (INVTRN) records
                    for all physical inventory detail (WHSPID) records in which
                    a variance between the "freeze" count and the entered count
                    exists.
         WPI023     Assign Sections
                    This program reads a workfile that contains Store, From
                    Slot, To Slot and Section Number.  All slots in the From-To
                    range are read and assigned the new section number.
         WPI021     Purge a Physical Inventory Batch
                    This program purges all WHSPID, WHSPIX and WHSPIZ for the
                    batch selected and then purges the batch header record,
                    WHSPIH.
                                   *** END OF REPORT ***
                                 MEMBER: DOCRTV1                 PAGE:   1
         ==================================================================
                             RETURN TO VENDOR MODULE
         ==================================================================
                           Operates on: IBM AS/400
                  Languages written in: RPG400 & CL
                       First Installed: 1987
         The Return To Vendor module offers the ability to return defective
         goods, receive and store the goods and then selectively or collectively
         choose items to be returned to the vendor.  This module keeps track
         of inventory movement in the defective warehouse and creates all
         necessary paper work to obtain authorization and to eventually
         ship the merchandise back to the vendor.
                                 MEMBER: DOCRTV1                 PAGE:   2
         ==================================================================
                                  FEATURES
         ==================================================================
         * Detailed inquiry and reporting of RTVs at each stage of the
           inventory movement process from work status to complete status.
         * Has ability to tie in original RTV documents with the moves
           being performed in the warehouse.
         * Keeps track of inventory locations for inventory contained in the
           Returns Goods Memo until vendor authorization has been received.
         * Allows user to select all inventory for a vendor to be returned,
           automatically allocating goods and creating Return Goods Memo or
           to selectively choose merchandise to be returned.
         *
                                 MEMBER: DOCRTV1                 PAGE:   3
         ==================================================================
                          STANDARD REPORTS & INQUIRIES
         ==================================================================
         Screen Inquiries:
         RTV023    General RTV Inquiry
         RTV011    Unshipped RTV Inquiry
         RTV017    Inquiry RTV's By Return Type
         WHS023    Slot Skus Within Locations
         Standard Reports:
         RTV024    Damaged/Defective Skus Report
         RTV028    Vendor Product Line Report
         RTV038    Vendor Sku Location Listing
         RTV048    Vendor Return Summary Report
         RTV042    Return Goods Memo
         RTV043    Return Goods Memo For Accounts Payable
                                 MEMBER: DOCRTV1                 PAGE:   4
         ==================================================================
                                 SUBPROGRAMS USED
         ==================================================================
         GETCST     Gets appropriate cost for SKU depending on Costing Method
                    defined in TBLSTR.
         PRC003     Used to get appropriate retail price for SKU.  Chaining to
                    IPRICE does not obtain the correct price.
         MENU16     Used to get values from the OPTIONS file in a control
                    language program.
                                 MEMBER: DOCRTV1                 PAGE:   5

         Table Files:
         INVDAT     Current Date File
                    Contains current date information such as current period,
                    week of period, week of year, current year, current day
                    and a literal description of the date.
         TBLZON     Warehouse Zone File
                    Table of valid zones by store with such information as
                    zone name, zone type, and whether it is the primary zone,
                    etc.
         WHSCLK     Warehouse Clerk File
                    Table of valid clerks for the a store and their name.
         INVUMR     Inventory Unit Of Measure
                    Table of valid units of measure with a description and
                    a sell/buy factor.
         OPTIONS    Special Processing Option File
         TBLSTR     Table of Valid Stores
         MENUCTL    Menu Control File Name
         Master Files:
         INVMST     Inventory Master File
                    Contains such sku information as description, department,
                    sub-department, class, sub-class and cost information.
         INVBAL     Inventory Balance Files
                    Keeps inventory balance and movement data by store/sku.
         IWHSTR     Warehouse Shipment Transactions
                    Warehouse transaction file that contains the transaction
                    code, year and week of transaction and transaction quantity.
         IWHSHP     Warehouse Shipment History
                    Warehouse/Sku weekly shipments containing current and
                    previous shipping quantities.
         Transaction Files:
         WHSMVH     Warehouse Move Header File
                    Contains one record for every move document number.
         WHSMVD     Warehouse Move Detail File
                    Keeps move detail information such as quantity requested
                    and quantity moved.
         WHSMVX     Warehouse Move Cross Reference
                    Item/Location move cross reference containing the move
                    status and source document number.
         WHSMVS     Warehouse Move Document To Store Picking Information
                    Contains to location, total labels and total cube.
         TRFHDR     Transfer Header File
                    Contains one record for every transfer batch with the
                    batch name, status, from and to location and other header
                    information.
         TRFDTL     Transfer Detail File
                    Contains sku level transaction information like quantity
                    requested, shipped, received and allocated.
         TRFEXC     Transfer Interface Exception File
                    Contains sku ordered, exception code and the replaced or
                    substituted sku.
                                 MEMBER: DOCRTV1                 PAGE:   7
         TRFINT     Transfer Interface File
                    Contains store, sku, requested quantity, retail out and
                    minimum order quantity.
         TRFSSH     Transfer Sku Set Header
                    Contains one record for every sku set with the sku set
                    name and description.
         TRFSSD     Transfer Sku Set Detail
                    Sku set detail information, sku set name, sku and sequence
                    number.
         RTVHDR     Return To Vendor Header File
                    Contains one record for every RTV batch with the
                    batch name, status, from and to location and other header
                    information.
         RTVDTL     Return To Vendor Detail File
                    Contains sku level transaction information like quantity
                    requested, shipped, received and allocated.
         RTVSLC     Return To Vendor Sku Location File
                    Contains the allocation amount for a sku in a particular
                    location.
         Work Files:
         WRKMOV     Work Move File
                    Work file for building move transactions.
         LIMITS     General Purpose Limits File
         INVTRN     Inventory Transaction File
         WORKFILE   Workfile (Limits type) File
                                 MEMBER: DOCRTV1                 PAGE:   8

                                 MEMBER: DOCRTV1                 PAGE:  22
         =======================================================================
                             MAJOR PROGRAM DESCRIPTIONS
         =======================================================================
         TRF059     Return Merchandise To Stock
                    This program updates quantity requested, allocated, and
                    shipped in the transfer detail. An inventory transaction
                    is written to update store and warehouse onhands in the
                    inventory update program, INV038.  The quantities will
                    automatically be received into the default receiving slot
                    for the defective warehouse.
         RTV007     Allocate RTVs
                    The program is used to allocate and de-allocate inventory
                    in the WHSSKU file.  Sub-program RTV047 is used to actually
                    update the allocated in the warehouse sku master (WHSSKU)
                    file. The process will allocate up to the amount requested
                    depending on the amount available to be allocated.  The
                    amount available to be allocated is computed as follows:
                       On hand (from INVBAL file) less quantity in default
                       receiving slot less quantity already allocated less
                       quantity reserved.
         RTV015     Pick Merchandise For RTVs       (Step 1)
                    This program is the first step in the Pick RTV
                    Process.  It is executed by selecting and submitting
                    RTVs to be picked at which time the status of the RTV
                    is changed to "Release To Pick".
                    It's purpose is to create Work Move records (WRKMOV) and
                    commit the inventory from the sku locations selected for
                    allocation (or "all" sku locations if "all" merchandise for
                    a vendor will be returned). Sub-program TRF046 is called to
                    update the allocated in the warehouse sku master (WHSSKU)
                    file.  A wrkmov record is created for each sku/location
                    to be picked.  Sku cost is obtained at this time and updated
                    in the RTV detail record.
         RTV026     Pick Merchandise For RTVs   (Step 2)
                    This program is the second step in the Pick RTVs
                    Process.  It's purpose is to read the Work Move records
                    written during the first step (WHS010) by Zone, Pick Sort
                    Sequence, and From Location and create the Move Header
                    and Move Detail records for the Pick RTV Moves.
                    The RTV Header status is changed to "Picked" status at
                    this time.
                                 MEMBER: DOCRTV1                 PAGE:  23
         =======================================================================
                          MAJOR PROGRAM DESCRIPTIONS - cont'd
         =======================================================================
         RTV035     Cancel An RTV
                    The cancel process can only be initiated for an RTV
                    that has either a Work status or an Allocated status. The
                    process then de-allocates the inventory (if the RTV was
                    allocated) and deletes the RTV and it's associated detail
                    records.  Sub-program TRF046 actually updates the allocated
                    in the warehouse sku master (WHSSKU) file.
         RTV010     Ship RTVs
                    The program is used to ship RTVs either from a store or
                    a warehouse. Quantity shipped is updated in the detail
                    records and inventory transactions are written to update
                    inventory and relieve sku allocations. These inventory
                    transactions are input to the inventory update program,
                    INV038.
         RTV037     Purge RTVs
                    This program purges those RTVs that have a status of
                    Complete and a receive date less than or equal to the
                    date entered on the Purge RTV screen. An audit trail
                    of purged records is printed.
         INV038     Major Inventory Update Program
                    This program plays a crucial role in Warehouse
                    Inventory Control.  It is responsible for actually updating
                    the quantities of warehouse locations (except in the case of
                    Internal Warehouse Moves) and updating the warehouse
                    perpetual inventory.  It relieves committed and pending,
                    quantity allocated for a SKU, computes location cube, and
                    records shipment history in IWHSHP ( Warehouse Shipments
                    History) file.  An option exists allowing the user to select
                    a listing of all INVTRN transactions which have been created
                    for the process (INV041 program).
                                   *** END OF REPORT ***
                                 MEMBER: DOCCS                   PAGE:   1
         ==================================================================
                    C O U N T   S Y S T E M   A P P L I C A T I O N
                                O V E R V I E W
         ==================================================================
                           Operates on: IBM AS/400
                  Languages written in: RPG400 & CL
                       First Installed: 1987.
                                FEATURES
                                ========
         Preparation of Count Worksheet by:
                          Store (All or one)
                          Vendor (one)
                          Department,sub-department  (one)
                          Class,sub-class (any or all)
         Count Worksheet for count recording
         Ability to reprint count worksheet.
         Automatic count number generation.
         Easy count entry screen.
         Count search and inquiry.
         Count proof list.
         Ability to delete a count.
         Automatic generation of 'imputed' sales.
         Ability to enter and record receipts, transfers etc.
         Count history inquiry on 'imputed' sales and/or all other
         inventory transactions.
         Purge of transactions based on date.

                                 MEMBER: DOCCC                   PAGE:   1
         ======================================================================
                     C Y C L E   C O U N T   A P P L I C A T I O N
                                    O V E R V I E W
         ======================================================================
                                       FEATURES
                                       ========
         Provides ability to select SKU's for counts by:
                    - Location Range
                    - Vendor/Department/Sub-Dept/Class/Sub-Class
                    - SKU Range
         Provides ability to freeze inventory values prior to physical count.
         Permits counts from three(3) different locations.
         Provides ability to print inventory count sheets in location order.
         Produces an Inventory Discrepency Report (Book vs. Physical).
                    - All Items
                    - Only items with a Difference
         Produces an Inventory Stock Ledger.
         Maintains an Audit Trail of all inventory adjustments.

                                 MEMBER: PRICING                 PAGE:   1
            - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -
                 P R I C I N G   L O G I C   D E S C R I P T I O N
            - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -
            1. MAKING PRICE CHANGES.
                RECORDS IN 'IPRICE' CAN BE AFFECTED IN THREE WAYS:
                   A/ INV001 SKU MAINTENANCE PROGRAM - 000 RECORD ONLY.
                   B/ AD PLANNING MODULE, BASED ON EFFECTIVE DATE
                      FOR 000 RECORD, OR ONE STORE ONLY
                   C/ PRICE CHANGE MODULE, ALLOWS CHANGES TO 000 RECORD
                      OR ONE STORE.
             THIS PROGRAM (PRC001) WILL PASS THROUGH THE ENTIRE PRICE
             FILE ON A NIGHTLY BASIS, AND DECIDE WHETHER A PRICE
             ENTERED IN ANY OF THE ABOVE MANNERS IS NOW EFFECTIVE, BASED
             ON TODAYS DATE. IF IT IS, A 'P' IS PLACED IN THE FIELD
             'POSFLG'. IF, AT THE END OF DEALING WITH EACH SKU, THE
             PRICE CHANGE FLAG IS ON, IT MEANS THAT THERE NEEDS TO BE
             A PRICE CHANGE/ADDITION SENT TO THE POS EQUIPMENT.
             THE PROGRAM WILL UPDATE THE FILE 'INVSTR' WITH A 'P'
             IN IT'S RECORD.
             BY STORE:
             PROGRAM 'DTS010' WILL, FOR PROMPTED STORE, PASS THROUGH
             THE FILE 'INVSTR', LOOKING ONLY AT RECORDS THAT HAVE
             A 'P', PLACED THERE BY THIS PROGRAM, AND WILL CREATE THE
             APPROPRIATE RECORDS IN DTSOUTF AND DTSCMDF TO SEND TO
             THE POS DEVICE.  DEPENDING ON WHETHER OR NOT THIS SKU
             IS ON THE POS FOR THIS STORE (ISNEW = ' '), THE PROGRAM
             WILL DETERMINE ADD OR CHANGE.
             AFTER THE RECORD IS CREATED TO GO TO POS, THE INVSTR
             FILE HAS THE 'P' REMOVED AND ADDED RECORDS HAVE
             THE FIELD 'ISNEW' SET TO 'Y'.
            ----------------------------------------------------------
                        PRICING ROUTINE
              1.IF REGULAR EFFECTIVE DATE TODAY OR EARLIER,
                    A. MOVE NEXT REG PRICE TO CUR POS PRICE
                    B. MOVE CUR  REG PRICE TO LAST REG PRICE
                    C. MOVE NEXT REG EFF. DATE TO DATE LAST CHANGED
                    D. MOVE NEXT REG PRICE TO CUR REG PRICE
                    E. MAKE NEXT REG PRICE 0
                    F. MAKE REG EFFECTIVE DATE 0
                    G. MOVE 'P' TO POSFLG
                    H. DO PERMANANT MARKDOWN/MARKUP ROUTINE
              2. IF TODAY IS EQUAL TO OR GREATER THAN EFFECTIVE FROM DATE
                 AND LESS THAN OR EQUAL TO EFFECTIVE TO DATE,
                 AND NEXT AD PRICE IS NOT EQUAL TO CUR POS PRICE,
                    A. MOVE NEXT AD  PRICE TO CUR POS PRICE
                    B. MOVE 'P' TO POSFLG
                    C. MOVE 'A' TO AD ACTIVE FLAG
                                 MEMBER: PRICING                 PAGE:   2
                    D. SET MARKUP/DOWN $'S TO ZERO
              3. IF AD PRICE IS GT 0, AND TODAY IS OR GT ADD
                 EFFECTIVE TO (DEAL END) DATE,
                    A. MOVE CUR REG  PRICE TO CUR POS PRICE
                    B. MOVE ADD EFF DATE TO AD DATE LAST CHANGED
                    C. MOVE NEXT AD PRICE TO LAST ADD PRICE
                    D. MOVE 'P' TO POSFLG
                    D. MOVE ' ' TO POSFLG ADD ACTIVE FLAG
                    E. MOVE 0'S TO NEXT AD PRICE, EFFECTIVE DATES
                       AND AD NUMBER.
                    F. DO TEMPORARY MARKDOWN/MARKUP ROUTINE
                    G. SET MARKUP/DOWN $'S TO ZERO
            * NOTE: SALES AUDIT SYSTEM WILL CHECK FOR ACTIVE
                    AD'S AND ACCUMULATE MRKUP/DOWN $'S
            ----------------------------------------------------------
                                   *** END OF REPORT ***
                                 MEMBER: POLLING                 PAGE:   1
              -----------------------------------------------------
                           EXPLANATION OF POLLING
              -----------------------------------------------------
                STEP 1. ANY JOBS THAT ARE TO BE RUN AFTER POLLING
                        PROCESSING ARE TO BE PLACED IN THE JOB Q
                        'NIGHTQ'. THIS JOBQ WILL BE ON HOLD
                        DURING THE DAY. THE ARRIVAL OF EACH OF
                        THESE JOBS IS DETERMINED BY THAT JOB'S
                        INITIATION.
                STEP 2. JOB 'POLLSTRT' IS RUN ONE OF TWO WAYS.
                        A/ AT THE END OF EACH NIGHTS POLLING
                           PROCESS, JOB POLLEND WILL SUBMIT THE
                           'POLLSTRT' JOB. IN OTHER WORDS, IT IS
                           SELF PERPUTATING UNTIL THE MACHINE IS
                           TURNED OFF, OR THE JOB 'POLLWAIT' IS
                           OTHERWISE CANCELLED.
                        B/ IF, FOR SOME REASON, THE POLLING
                           PROCESS MUST BE STARTED MANUALLY, THE
                           'POLLSTRT' JOB CAN BE INITIATED OFF OF
                           THE POLLING MENU (MENPOL). IT SHOULD
                           ONLY BE STARTED IF POLLING IS NOT
                           ALREADY RUNNING IN THE SUBSYSTEM
                           'QBATCH' OR IN THE JOBQ 'POLLQ'.
                STEP 3. JOB 'POLLSTRT' SUBMITS JOB 'POLLWAIT'
                        TO JOBQ 'POLLQ.'
                STEP 4. JOB 'POLLWAIT' FIRST WAITS UNTIL AT LEAST
                        6 PM IN THE EVENING. IF IT IS AFTER
                        6 PM, IT WILL RUN PROGRAM POLL02, WHICH
                        DETERMINES THE CURRENT DATE FROM THE
                        POLLING DATE FILE 'POLLDATE'. THE ACCURACY
                        OF THIS FILE IS ABSOLUTELY CRITICAL. THEN
                        PROGRAM POLL03 STARTS. THIS PROGRAM WILL
                        LOOK EVERY TEN MINUTES TO SEE WHETHER
                        ONE OF THE FOLLOWING HAS HAPPENED:
                         A/ THE TIME TO START POLLING FOR THIS
                            PARTICULAR DAY HAS COME.
                                 OR
                         B/ SOMEONE HAS REQUESTED THAT POLLING
                            START IMMEDIATELY.
                                 MEMBER: POLLING                 PAGE:   2
                        THE OPTIONS FILE IS USED IN THE ABOVE
                        PROCESS AS FOLLOWS
                        RECORD
                        NUMBER  CONTROLLING DATA
                         1050   SHOULD POLLING START EARLY ?
                         1051   TIME TO START POLLING - SUNDAY
                         1052   TIME TO START POLLING - MONDAY
                         1053   TIME TO START POLLING - TUEDAY
                         1054   TIME TO START POLLING - WEDNESDAY
                         1055   TIME TO START POLLING - THURSDAY
                         1056   TIME TO START POLLING - FRIDAY
                         1057   TIME TO START POLLING - SATURDAY
                STEP 5. WHEN JOB 'POLLWAIT' HAS FINISHED WAITING,
                        IT SENDS A MESSAGE TO QSYSOPR STATING THAT
                        'POLLING PROGRAM HAS AWAKENED.'
                        IT THEN SUBMITS TO JOBQ 'POLLQ' THE
                        FOLLOWING TWO JOBS.
                        POLLRUN - RUN THE ACTUAL POLLING PROCESSES.
                        POLLHOLD - SUBMIT A JOB TO JOBQ 'NIGHTQ'
                                   WHICH PUTS JOBQ 'NIGHTQ' ON
                                   HOLD. THIS WILL BE THE LAST
                                   JOB IN 'NIGHTQ'. THE POLLING
                                   PROCESS WILL, AS A LAST STEP,
                                   RELEASE THE 'NIGHTQ' SO THAT
                                   THE SUBMITTED DESCRIBED IN
                                   STEP ONE ABOVE WILL RUN. THE
                                   LAST STEP, OF COURSE, IS TO
                                   PUT THE JOBQ ON HOLD SO THAT
                                   ANYTHING PUT IN THERE TOMMORROW
                                   WILL BE ON HOLD UNTIL NIGHT TIME
               STEP 6. THE 'POLLRUN' PROCESS.
               STEP 6A.FIRST STEP IN POLLING RUN IS TO WALK THROUGH
                       THE PRICE FILE AND DETERMINE WHETHER OR
                       NOT PRICES HAVE BECOME EFFECTIVE. IF THE
                       SKU REQUIRES A PRICE CHANGE, BASED ON THE
                       EFFECTIVE DATES (REGULAR OR AD), THEN THE
                       RECORD IS UPDATED WITH THE NEW POS PRICE,
                       AND THE APPROPRIATE RECORDS ARE UPDATED
                       IN THE 'INVSTR' FILE, INDICATING THAT A
                       PRICE CHANGE IS 'PENDING' FOR THIS SKU IN
                       THIS STORE.
                                 MEMBER: POLLING                 PAGE:   3
               STEP 6B.THE NEXT STEP IS TO DETERMINE, IN PROGRAM
                       'POLL05', WHICH STORES IN THE STORE TABLE
                       FILE (TBLSTR) ARE: 1) STORES (NOT HEAD
                       OFFICES OR WAREHOUSES) AND 2) HAVE POS
                       ACTIVE. FOR EACH OF THESE, A JOB CALLED
                       SETUPXXX (XXX IS STORE NUMBER) IS SUBMITTED
                       TO THE JOBQ 'POLLQ'
               STEP 6C.USING THE SAME PROGRAM (POLL05) AND THE
                       SAME LOGIC AS 6B, A JOB CALLED DIALXXX IS
                       SET UP IN 'POLLQ' FOR EACH ACTIVE STORE.
               STEP 6D.A JOB CALLED 'POLLEND' IS SUBMITTED TO
                       'POLLQ'. THIS JOB WILL RUN AFTER THE
                       SETUP, DIAL, AND PROC RUNS ARE COMPLETE.
                       THIS JOB IS DESCRIBED BELOW.
                CONTENTS OF POLLING JOB QUEUE  (POLLQ)
                AFTER 'POLLRUN', THE JOB QUEUE LOOKS THUSLY:
                      SETUP001
                      SETUP002
                      SETUP003 ...ETC.
                      DIAL001
                      DIAL002
                      DIAL003  ...ETC.
                      POLLEND
               SETUPXXX: CREATES THE FOLLOWING FILES
                         DTSCMDF - COMMANDS TO BE SENT TO THE
                                   SIERRA REQUESTING:
                                   A) RECEIVE PRICE CHANGES
                                   B) SEND REPORTS:
                                       018 - ITEM MOVEMENT
                                       085 - CASHIER REPORT
                         DTSINPF - PRICE CHANGES/ADDITIONS/DELETES
                         DTSRESP - BLANK FILE TO CONTAIN COMMUNIC-
                                   ATIONS RESPONSES.
                         DTSOUTF - BLANK FILE TO CONTAIN DATA
                                   RECEIVED FROM SIERRA
                         WITH THE EXECPTION OF 'DTSRESP', THESE
                         FILES ARE 'MEMBERED' BY STORE (S001 E.G.)
                                 MEMBER: POLLING                 PAGE:   4
               DIALXXX: WILL RETRIEVE THE TELEPHONE NUMBER FOR
                        THIS STORE FROM THE STORE TABLE FILE
                        (TBLSTR) AND CHANGE THE CONTROL UNIT TO
                        USE IT. IT WILL THEN RUN PROGRAM
                        'DTSTALK', WHICH WILL ATTEMPT UP TO
                        THREE TIMES TO ESTABLISH COMMUNICATIONS
                        WITH THE STORE LEVEL SIERRA.
                        IF COMMUNICATIONS IS NOT ESTABLISHED, OR
                        IF IT IS ESTABLISHED BUT THE TRANSMISSION
                        IS IMCOMPLETE, NO FURTHER PROCESSING IS
                        DONE FOR THIS STORE. ERROR WARNING
                        MESSAGES ARE SENT TO THE SYSTEM OPERATOR.
                        IF IT IS COMPLETE, DTSTALK WILL SUBMIT
                        JOB 'PROCXXX'
               PROCXXX: THIS JOB WILL PROCESS THE INFORMATION
                        RECEIVED FROM THE SIERRA.
               NOTE: EVEN THOUGH PROGRAM 'POLLEND' WAS SUBMITTED
                     BEFORE 'PROCXXX',IT WAS GIVEN THE LOWEST
                     PRIORITY, SO IT WILL RUN DEAD LAST.
               POLLEND : THIS PROGRAM WILL DO THE FOLLOWING
                         1) INCREMENT THE DATE IN 'POLLDATE'
                            BY ONE DAY.
                         2) CLEAR ANY JOBS FROM 'BBATCH'
                         3) SUBMIT 'BACKNIGHT' - THE BACKUP
                            TO DISK.
                         4) WAIT UNTIL AT LEAST 1 A.M.
                         5) SUBMIT POLLSTRT
                PROCESS NOW BEGINS ALL OVER AGAIN.
                                   *** END OF REPORT ***
